====================================================================================================
KDP LEGACY CORE (PSEUDONYMISED) - SINGLE FILE BUNDLE
====================================================================================================
Stack: .NET Framework 4.5.1, WinForms, EF6 on Oracle, AutoMapper, WCF service contracts, Janus controls.
Call chain: screen -> delegate -> service -> unit of work / EF context -> Oracle (schema APPDATA1).
Each file below starts with a banner:  >>> FILE n/N: <path>   [layer]  and ends with  <<< END FILE.
Search for the string  >>> FILE  to jump between files.
Not a buildable project: only the files on this path are included, with unrelated members removed.

====================================================================================================
>>> FILE 1/94: HLX.Net2008/BooleanToColorConverter.cs   [1. UI (WinForms screen)]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace HLX.Net2008
{
    public class BooleanToColorConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            var colors = parameter as Color[];
            if (colors == null) return null;
            return (bool)value ? colors[1] : colors[0];
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 1/94: HLX.Net2008/BooleanToColorConverter.cs

====================================================================================================
>>> FILE 2/94: HLX.Net2008/Common Classes/PopulateDropDown.cs   [1. UI (WinForms screen)]
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Janus.Windows.EditControls;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;

namespace HLX.Net2008
{
    public static class PopulateDropDown
    {
        public static void Populate(UIComboBox combBox, List<LookupsSubTO> _list, bool addNullRow)
        {
            combBox.Items.Clear();
            foreach (var item in _list) { combBox.Items.Add(item.Description, item.Code); }
            if (addNullRow) combBox.Items.Add("", null);
        }

        public static void Populate(UIComboBox combBox, Dictionary<string, byte?> values)
        {
            if (combBox.Items.Count == 0)
                foreach (var item in values) { combBox.Items.Add(item.Key, item.Value); }
        }

        public static void Populate(UIComboBox combBox, Dictionary<string, string> values)
        {
            if (combBox.Items.Count == 0)
                foreach (var item in values) { combBox.Items.Add(item.Key, item.Value); }
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 2/94: HLX.Net2008/Common Classes/PopulateDropDown.cs

using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.ViewModels;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace HLX.Net2008
{
    // ReSharper disable once InconsistentNaming
    public partial class ucHLXKdpExaminationResult : UserControl
    {
        private KdpExaminationResultDelegate kdpExaminationResultDelegate;
        private bool dropDownsPopulated;

        public ucHLXKdpExaminationResult()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            new BooleanToColorConverter().Bind(labelCalculatedBMI, "ForeColor",
                bindingSourceKdpExaminationResults, "IsBmiOutsideRange", new[] { Color.Black, Color.Red });

            this.Show();            
        }

        public void Populate(KdpExaminationResultDelegate kdpExaminationResultDelegate)
        {
            this.kdpExaminationResultDelegate = kdpExaminationResultDelegate;
            bindingSourceKdpExaminationResults.DataSource = this.kdpExaminationResultDelegate;

            if (dropDownsPopulated)
                return;

            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownExaminerType, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ExaminerTypeList, true);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownLocationType, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.LocationTypeList, true);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownOrigin, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.OriginList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownReasonNotExamined, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.NotExaminedReasonList, true);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownConsent, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownOnwardReferrals, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownBoostScheme, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoUnkownIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownLookedAfterChild, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoUnkownIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownChildProtection, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoUnkownIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownEvidenceOfXgm, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.EvidenceOfXgmList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDomesticViolence, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DomesticViolenceConcernList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownResponsiveParent, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.RskScores);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownEngagement, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.RskScores);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownSocioFactors, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.RskScores);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownFamilyHealth, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.RskScores);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownFamilySupport, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.RskScores);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownRskComplete, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDevelopment, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DevelopmentAt15And27MonthsList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDevelopmentFurther, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.YesNoIncludeNull);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownHeart, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.PhysicalAssessment6To8WeeksList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownHips, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.PhysicalAssessment6To8WeeksList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownEyes, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.PhysicalAssessment6To8WeeksList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownTestes, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.PhysicalAssessment6To8WeeksList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownPhysicalGeneral, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.PhysicalAssessment6To8WeeksList);
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskLocomotor, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.Locomotor));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskManipulative, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.Manipulative));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskVisual, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.Visual));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskHearing, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.Hearing));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskSpeech, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.Speech));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskInteractiveSocial, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.InteractiveSocial));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskSelfCareSocial, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.SelfCareSocial));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownDskCognitive, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk.Cognitive));
            PopulateDropDown.Populate(hlxDropDownCoreComponentOffered, kdpExaminationResultDelegate.CoreComponentOfferedList);

            dropDownsPopulated = true;
            this.hlxDropDownExaminerType.InvalidValues = this.kdpExaminationResultDelegate.InvalidKdpCodesList;
            this.hlxDropDownExaminerType.OriginalValue = this.kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ExaminerCategory;

            manuallyRefreshSelectedDropdownValues();
        }

        private void manuallyRefreshSelectedDropdownValues()
        {
            hlxDropDownChildProtection.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownChildProtection);            
            hlxDropDownReasonNotExamined.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownReasonNotExamined);
            hlxDropDownConsent.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownConsent);
            hlxDropDownCoreComponentOffered.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownCoreComponentOffered);
            hlxDropDownDevelopment.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDevelopment);
            hlxDropDownDevelopmentFurther.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDevelopmentFurther);
            hlxDropDownDomesticViolence.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDomesticViolence);
            hlxDropDownEngagement.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDomesticViolence);
            hlxDropDownEvidenceOfXgm.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownEvidenceOfXgm);
            hlxDropDownExaminerType.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownExaminerType);
            hlxDropDownEyes.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownEyes);
            hlxDropDownFamilyHealth.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownFamilyHealth);
            hlxDropDownFamilySupport.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownFamilySupport);
            hlxDropDownBoostScheme.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownBoostScheme);
            hlxDropDownRskComplete.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownRskComplete);
            hlxDropDownHeart.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownHeart);
            hlxDropDownHips.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownHips);
            hlxDropDownLocationType.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownLocationType);
            hlxDropDownLookedAfterChild.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownLookedAfterChild);
            hlxDropDownOnwardReferrals.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownOnwardReferrals);
            hlxDropDownOrigin.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownOrigin);
            hlxDropDownPhysicalGeneral.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownPhysicalGeneral);
            hlxDropDownResponsiveParent.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownResponsiveParent);
            hlxDropDownSocioFactors.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownSocioFactors);
            hlxDropDownDskCognitive.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskCognitive);
            hlxDropDownDskHearing.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskHearing);
            hlxDropDownDskInteractiveSocial.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskInteractiveSocial);
            hlxDropDownDskLocomotor.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskLocomotor);
            hlxDropDownDskManipulative.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskManipulative);
            hlxDropDownDskSelfCareSocial.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskSelfCareSocial);
            hlxDropDownDskSpeech.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskSpeech);
            hlxDropDownDskVisual.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownDskVisual);
            hlxDropDownTestes.SelectedIndex = getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(hlxDropDownTestes);
        }        

        private int getSelectedIndexOfSelectedValue(HlxDropDown dropDownBox)
        {
            int selectedIndexOfValue = -1;

            if (dropDownBox.SelectedValue != null)
            {
                int currentIndex = 0;
                foreach (Janus.Windows.EditControls.UIComboBoxItem item in dropDownBox.Items)
                {
                    if (item.Value.ToString() == dropDownBox.SelectedValue.ToString())
                    {
                        selectedIndexOfValue = currentIndex;
                        break;
                    }
                    currentIndex++;
                }
            }

            return selectedIndexOfValue;
        }

        private void hlxTextBoxExamCode_ButtonClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            kdpExaminationResultDelegate.LookUpExamCode();
        }

        private void hlxTextBoxExamCode_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            e.Cancel = !kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateAndSetExamCode(hlxTextBoxExamCode.Text);
            if (e.Cancel)
                kdpExaminationResultDelegate.LookUpExamCode();
        }

        private void hlxDateBoxExamDate_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hlxDateBoxExamDate.Text))
            {
                e.Cancel = !kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateExaminationDate(hlxDateBoxExamDate.Value);
            }
        }

        private void hlxNumericEditBoxCustomHeight_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hlxNumericEditBoxCustomHeight.Text))
            {
                e.Cancel = !kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateHeight((decimal?)hlxNumericEditBoxCustomHeight.Value);
            }
        }

        private void hlxNumericEditBoxCustomWeight_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hlxNumericEditBoxCustomWeight.Text))
            {
                e.Cancel = !kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateWeight((decimal?)hlxNumericEditBoxCustomWeight.Value);
            }
        }

        private void hlxNumericEditBoxCustomHeadCirc_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hlxNumericEditBoxCustomHeadCirc.Text))
            {
                e.Cancel = !kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateHeadCircumference((decimal)hlxNumericEditBoxCustomHeadCirc.Value);
            }
        }

        public void FocusHlxTextBoxExamCode()
        {
            if (hlxTextBoxExamCode.Text.Length == 0)
            {
                hlxTextBoxExamCode.Focus();
            }
        }

        private void ValidateExaminerTypeInput(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
        }

        private void ValidateLocationTypeInput(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateNumericDescriptorEntered(((HlxDropDown)sender).Text.Trim(), KdpExaminationResultDelegate.TYPE_OF_INPUT.LOCATON_TYPE))
            {
                ((HlxDropDown)sender).Text = "";
            };
        }

        private void ValidateExaminerTypeInput(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateNumericDescriptorEntered(((HlxDropDown)sender).Text.Trim(), KdpExaminationResultDelegate.TYPE_OF_INPUT.EXAMINER_TYPE))
            {
                ((HlxDropDown)sender).Text = "";
            };
        }

        private void ValidateReasonNotExaminedTypeInput(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!kdpExaminationResultDelegate.ValidateNumericDescriptorEntered(((HlxDropDown)sender).Text.Trim(), KdpExaminationResultDelegate.TYPE_OF_INPUT.REASON_NOT_EXAMINED))
            {
                ((HlxDropDown)sender).Text = "";
            };
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 4/94: HLX.Net2008/Data Input (HLX - Child Health)/Examination-Unscheduled/Kdp/ucHLXKdpExaminations.cs   [1. UI (WinForms screen)]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using Orvex.ViewModels;
using Janus.Windows.UI.Tab;

namespace HLX.Net2008
{
    public partial class ucHLXKdpExaminations :  HLXUserControl, IUserControl
    {
        private const string NewTabHeading = "New Result";
        private const string NewTabKey = "0";
        private const int NewExamNumber = 0;
        private UITabPage currentTab;
        private bool setDefaultTabPage =true;
        private bool processingTabs;
        private readonly KdpExaminationDelegate kdpExaminationDelegate;
        public ucHLXKdpExaminationResult UcHlxKdpExaminationResult { get; private set; }
        
        public bool IsPopulatedFromContactsSummary = false;

        public ucHLXKdpExaminations(KdpExaminationDelegate kdpExaminationDelegate)
        {
            this.kdpExaminationDelegate = kdpExaminationDelegate;
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public bool InDesignMode { get; }
        public bool Editable { get; set; }
        public decimal ID { get; set; }
        public decimal ChangesCount { get { return kdpExaminationDelegate.ChangesCount; } }

        public event EventHandler Deleted;
        public event EventHandler Saved;
        public void Populate(decimal RecordID, bool IsEditable)
        {
            Editable = IsEditable;
            ID = RecordID;
            kdpExaminationDelegate.GetKdpResults(Convert.ToInt64(ID));
            ProcessTabsForEachExam();
            SetUpTabPages();

            if (IsPopulatedFromContactsSummary) // Hide binding navigator if control populated from KDP Schedule 'Contacts Summary' tab
                bindingNavigatorKdpExamination.Visible = false;            
        }

        private void ProcessTabsForEachExam()
        {
            processingTabs = true;

            while (hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Count < kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates.Count)
            {
                hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Add(new UITabPage());
            }
            while (hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Count > kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates.Count)
            {
                hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.RemoveAt(hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Count - 1);
            }

            processingTabs = false;
        }
        private void SetUpTabPages()
        {
            if (hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Count > 0)
            {
                for (int result=0; result < kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates.Count;result++)
                {
                    if (kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates[result].PkChildExaminationId != NewExamNumber)
                    {
                        hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages[result].Key =kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates[result].PkChildExaminationId.ToString("D");
                        hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages[result].Text =kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates[result].ResultTabText;
                    }
                }
                SelectTabPage();
                SetUpTabPageContent(hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab);
            }
        }

        private void SelectTabPage()
        {
            hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab = setDefaultTabPage ? hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages[0] : currentTab;
        }

        private void SetUpTabPageContent(UITabPage tab)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tab?.Key))
            {
                var tabKey = Convert.ToInt64(tab.Key);
                if (tabKey != 0)
                {
                    tab.Controls.Clear();
                }
                var kdpExamResultDelegate = kdpExaminationDelegate.KdpExaminationResultDelegates.Find(d => d.PkChildExaminationId == tabKey);
                if (kdpExamResultDelegate != null)
                {
                    UcHlxKdpExaminationResult = new ucHLXKdpExaminationResult {Dock = DockStyle.Fill};
                    UcHlxKdpExaminationResult.Populate(kdpExamResultDelegate);
                    tab.Controls.Add(UcHlxKdpExaminationResult);
                    currentTab = tab;
                    UcHlxKdpExaminationResult.FocusHlxTextBoxExamCode();
                }
            }
        }

        private void hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination_ChangingSelectedTab(object sender, TabCancelEventArgs e)
        {
            if (processingTabs || hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab == null)
                return;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Page?.Key)  && kdpExaminationDelegate.ConfirmCancelChanges(Convert.ToInt64(hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab.Key)))
                SetUpTabPageContent(e.Page);
            else e.Cancel = true;
        }
        public bool SaveChanges()
        {
            currentTab = hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab;
            if (currentTab != null && kdpExaminationDelegate.Save(Convert.ToInt64(currentTab.Key)))
            {
                Populate(ID, Editable);
                setDefaultTabPage = false;
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        public void Delete()
        {
            if (hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab == null)
                return;

            var currentTabKey = hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab.Key;
            var currentTabText = hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.SelectedTab.Text;

            if (kdpExaminationDelegate.Delete(Convert.ToInt64(currentTabKey), (long)ID, currentTabText))
            {
                setDefaultTabPage = true;
                Populate(ID, Editable);
            }
        }

        public void AfterCancel()
        {
            
        }

        private void toolStripButtonInsert_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            setDefaultTabPage = true;
            if (hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Count > 0 && hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages[0].Key == NewTabKey)
                return;
            
            hlxAdminTabControlKdpExamination.TabPages.Insert(0, new UITabPage { Text = NewTabHeading, Key = NewTabKey });
            kdpExaminationDelegate.AddNewKdpResult();
            SetUpTabPages();
        }

        private void toolStripButtonSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Save();
        }

        private void toolStripButtonDelete_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Delete();
        }

        private void ucHLXKdpExaminations_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            setDefaultTabPage = false;
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 5/94: Orvex.ViewModels/KdpExaminationDelegate.cs   [2. Delegates / view models]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.ServiceModel;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using HLX.Net2008.Common;
using Orvex.Business.Validation;
using Orvex.Messages;
using Orvex.Messages.Faults;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;

namespace Orvex.ViewModels
{
    public class KdpExaminationDelegate
    {
        private List<KdpExaminationResultDelegate> listOfKdpExaminationResultDelegates;
        private KdpExaminationTO ato;
        private readonly IKdpExaminationService service;
        private readonly KdpExaminationValidator validator;
        private readonly IMessenger messenger;
        private readonly Func<KdpExaminationResultDelegate> resultDelegateFactory;

        public decimal ChangesCount { get { return listOfKdpExaminationResultDelegates.Sum(rd => rd.ChangesCount); } }
        
        public List<KdpExaminationResultDelegate> KdpExaminationResultDelegates => listOfKdpExaminationResultDelegates;

        public KdpExaminationDelegate(IKdpExaminationService service, KdpExaminationValidator validator, IMessenger messenger, Func<KdpExaminationResultDelegate> resultDelegateFactory)
        {
            this.service = service;
            this.validator = validator;
            this.messenger = messenger;
            this.resultDelegateFactory = resultDelegateFactory;
        }

        public void GetKdpResults(long clientId)
        {
            ato = service.GetKdpExamination(clientId);
            listOfKdpExaminationResultDelegates = ato.HcpExaminationResultToList.OrderByDescending(it => it.ExaminationDate).Select(GetNewKdpExaminationResultDelegate).ToList();
        }

        public KdpExaminationResultDelegate AddNewKdpResult()
        {
            try
            {
                validator.ValidateKdpConsent(Convert.ToInt64(ato.Id), ato.HasPreschoolConsent);
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationWarningFault> ex)
            {
                var warning = new WarningNotificationMessage(ex.Message, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, ex.Detail.Title);
                messenger.Send(warning);
            }

            var newResult = new KdpExaminationResultTO
            {
                ClientId = Convert.ToInt64(ato.Id),
                PkChildExaminationId = 0,
                ResultEnteredDate = DateTime.Now,
                Origin = (byte?)LookupsUtility.EXAMINATION_ORIGIN.UNSCHEDULED,
                Reschedule = HLXBoolean.False,
                HeightMeasurementDeclined = HLXBoolean.False,
                WeightMeasurementDeclined = HLXBoolean.False,
                HeadMeasurementDeclined = HLXBoolean.False,
                EnabledPropChangeEvents = true
            };

            

            foreach (var outstandingAppointment in ato.OutstandingAppointmentsList)
            {
                if (outstandingAppointment.ExaminationDate != null)
                {
                    var confirmationMessage = new YesNoConfirmationRequiredMessage(
                        string.Format(Resources.Resources.OutstandingAppointmentMessage,
                            outstandingAppointment.ExaminationCode, outstandingAppointment.ExaminationDate.Value.ToString("dd MMM yyyy")),
                        Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.OutstandingAppointmentExistsPrompt);
                    messenger.Send(confirmationMessage);

                    if (confirmationMessage.Response)
                    {
                        newResult.ExaminationCode = outstandingAppointment.ExaminationCode;
                        newResult.ExaminationDate = outstandingAppointment.ExaminationDate.Value;
                        newResult.Origin = (byte?)outstandingAppointment.Origin;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            var newResultDelegate = GetNewKdpExaminationResultDelegate(newResult);

            listOfKdpExaminationResultDelegates.Insert(0,newResultDelegate);
            return newResultDelegate;
        }
      
        private KdpExaminationResultDelegate GetNewKdpExaminationResultDelegate(KdpExaminationResultTO kdpExaminationResultTo)
        {
            var kdpResultDelegate = resultDelegateFactory();
            kdpResultDelegate.Populate(kdpExaminationResultTo, ato.Gender, ato.DateOfBirth, ato.DateOfDeath, ato.HcpExaminationResultToList);
            return kdpResultDelegate;
        }

        public bool Save(long kdpChildExaminationId)
        {
            try
            {
                var kdpExamResultToSave =
                    listOfKdpExaminationResultDelegates.Single(re => re.PkChildExaminationId == kdpChildExaminationId)
                        .KdpExaminationResultTo;
                service.SaveKdpExaminationResult(ato, kdpExamResultToSave);
                return true;
            }
            catch (FaultException<OptimisticLockFault> ex)
            {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ExamResultsErrorSavingPrompt);
                messenger.Send(error);
                return false;
            }
            catch (FaultException ex)
            {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ExamResultsErrorSavingPrompt);
                messenger.Send(error);
                return false;
            }
        }

        public bool ConfirmCancelChanges(long kdpChildExaminationId)
        {
            if (KdpExaminationResultDelegates.Count >0 && kdpChildExaminationId >0)
            {
                var kdpExamResultDelegate = KdpExaminationResultDelegates.Single(er => er.PkChildExaminationId == kdpChildExaminationId);
                if (kdpExamResultDelegate != null && kdpExamResultDelegate.KdpExaminationResultTo.hasChanged())
                {
                    var confirmation = new YesNoConfirmationRequiredMessage(Resources.Resources.ExamResultsConfirmCancelMessage, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ConfirmCancelPrompt);
                    messenger.Send(confirmation);
                    if (confirmation.Response)
                    {
                        kdpExamResultDelegate.KdpExaminationResultTo = service.GetKdpExaminationResult(kdpExamResultDelegate.KdpExaminationResultTo.PkChildExaminationId);
                        return true;
                    }
                    return false;
                }return true;
            }
            return true;
        }

        public bool Delete(long examinationId, long clientId, string examinationCode)
        {
            var confirmation = new YesNoConfirmationRequiredMessage(Resources.Resources.ConfirmContinue, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ConfirmDeletionPrompt);
            messenger.Send(confirmation);

            if (!confirmation.Response)
                return false;

            if (examinationId > 0)
            {
                try
                {
                    service.DeleteKdpExaminationResult(examinationId, clientId, examinationCode);
                }
                catch (FaultException<OptimisticLockFault>)
                {
                    messenger.Send(new WarningNotificationMessage(Resources.Resources.OptimisticLockOnDeleteMessage, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.WarningPrompt));
                }
                catch (FaultException ex)
                {
                    messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, Resources.Resources.KdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ExamResultsErrorDeletingPrompt));
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }
    }
}

        public KdpExaminationResultDelegate(KdpExaminationValidator validator, IMessenger messenger, ILookupService lookupsService, IChildMeasurementsService measurementsService, IKdpExaminationService service)
        {
            this.validator = validator;
            this.lookupsService = lookupsService;
            Messenger = messenger;
            this.measurementsService = measurementsService;
            KdpExaminationService = service;

            kdpExaminationList = new Lazy<List<LookupsSubTO>>(() => lookupsService.getLookups(TypeOfLookup.AllExams, string.Empty, ExamType.Kdp.ToString()));
            prescribedRanges = new Lazy<List<ChildMeasurementsRange>>(measurementsService.GetPrescribedRanges);
            locationTypesList = new Lazy<List<LookupsSubTO>>(() => lookupsService.getLookups(TypeOfLookup.ExamLocationTypeKdp, string.Empty, string.Empty));
            examinerTypesList = new Lazy<List<LookupsSubTO>>(() => lookupsService.getLookups(TypeOfLookup.ExaminerByPrimaryKeyKdp, string.Empty, string.Empty));
            notExaminedReasonList = new Lazy<List<LookupsSubTO>>(() => lookupsService.getLookups(TypeOfLookup.ExamNotExaminedReasonKdp, string.Empty, string.Empty), true);

            PropertyChanged += CheckBmi;
            InvalidKdpCodesList = ExaminerTypeCodePositions.Where(x => !("18,19,26,28".Split(',')).Contains( x.TypeCode.ToString())).Select(invalid=> invalid.TypeCode.ToString() ).ToList();
        }

        public void Populate(KdpExaminationResultTO kdpExaminationResultTo, string gender, DateTime dateOfBirth, DateTime? dateOfDeath, List<KdpExaminationResultTO> existingResults, bool isScheduled = false)
        {
            KdpExaminationResultTo = kdpExaminationResultTo;
            this.gender = gender;
            this.dateOfDeath = dateOfDeath;
            this.dateOfBirth = dateOfBirth;
            this.existingResults = existingResults;
            ResetDeviations();
            ResetRecommendedAgeRange();
            IsScheduled = isScheduled;
            UpdateExamDescription();
            PrependNumericsUiExaminerDescriptions();
            PrependNumericsUiLocationTypeDescriptions();
            PrependNumericsUiReasonNotExaminedDescriptions();
        }

        public Dictionary<string, string> DskScores(LookupsUtility.Dsk dskSkill)
        {
            dskScoreMaximums = dskScoreMaximums ?? new Dictionary<LookupsUtility.Dsk, int>
            {
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.Locomotor, 20},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.Manipulative, 28},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.Visual, 20},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.Hearing, 21},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.Speech, 22},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.InteractiveSocial, 24},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.SelfCareSocial, 23},
                {LookupsUtility.Dsk.Cognitive, 34}
            };
            var dict = Enumerable.Range(0, dskScoreMaximums[dskSkill] + 1).Select(n => n.ToString()).ToDictionary(i => i);
            dict.Add(KdpResultsNotAssessedDescription, NotAssessedCode);
            return dict;
        }

        public Dictionary<string, byte?> RskScores => Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(n => (byte?)n).ToDictionary(s => s.ToString());

        {
            get { return KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode; }
            set
            {

                    return;

                KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode = value;
                UpdateExamDescription();
                RaisePropertyChanged(nameof(Is15Or27MonthContact));
                RaisePropertyChanged(nameof(Is6To8WeekContact));
                RaisePropertyChanged(nameof(IsDskContact));
                RaisePropertyChanged(nameof(IsRskContact));
                ClearDataFromHiddenFields();
                ResetRecommendedAgeRange();
            }
        }

        private void UpdateExamDescription()
        {
            KdpExaminationResultTo.ExamDescription = KdpExaminationList
                .SingleOrDefault(ex => ex.Code == KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode)
                    ?.Description;

            if (KdpExaminationResultTo.IsRepeat && KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode == KdpExaminationResultTo.OriginalExamCode
                && KdpExaminationResultTo.Lock == null)
                KdpExaminationResultTo.ExamDescription += RecallText;

            RaisePropertyChanged(nameof(ExamDescription));
        }

        private void PopulateHcpDetails()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminerHcpCode))
            {
                ExaminerName = string.Empty;
                ExaminerCategory = string.Empty;
            }
            else
            {
                var hcp = examinerList.FirstOrDefault(ex => ex.UsedCode == HLXBoolean.True && ex.Code == ExaminerKdpCode);
                ExaminerName = hcp?.Description;
                ExaminerType = hcp?.Type;
            }
        }

                KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminerName = string.Empty;
                examinerList = null;
            }

        public decimal BmiLowerBound => (decimal)prescribedRanges.Value.SingleOrDefault(d => d.MeasurementType == BmiMeasurmentType).LowerBound;
        public decimal BmiUpperBound => (decimal)prescribedRanges.Value.SingleOrDefault(d => d.MeasurementType == BmiMeasurmentType).UpperBound;
        public decimal? Bmi => (Height.HasValue && Weight.HasValue) ? measurementsService.CalculateBmi(Height.Value, Weight.Value) : null;
        public bool IsBmiOutsideRange => Bmi.HasValue && (Bmi < BmiLowerBound || BmiUpperBound < Bmi);

        public string OnChildProtectionRegister

        public bool ValidateAndSetExamCode(string examCode)
        {
            ExamCodeValidated = false;

            try
            {
                validator.ValidateExamCodeForDate(existingResults, PkChildExaminationId, examCode, ExaminationDate);
                validator.ValidateNewExamCode(examCode, KdpExaminationList);

                if (KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode != examCode)
                {
                    validator.ValidateExamCodeForDuplicates(existingResults, examCode, PkChildExaminationId);
                }
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationWarningFault> ex)
            {
                var confirmation = new YesNoConfirmationRequiredMessage(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ex.Detail.Title);
                Messenger.Send(confirmation);
                if (!confirmation.Response)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> ex)
            {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ex.Detail.Title);
                Messenger.Send(error);
                return false;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode) && KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminationCode != examCode)
            {
                var confirmation = new YesNoConfirmationRequiredMessage(KdpResultsExamCodeChangeConfirmationMessage, KdpResultsTitle, KdpResultsExamCodeChangeConfirmationPrompt);
                Messenger.Send(confirmation);
                if (!confirmation.Response)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            ExamCodeValidated = true;
            ExaminationCode = examCode;
            return true;
        }

        public bool ValidateExaminationDate(DateTime examDate)
        {
            try
            {
                validator.ValidateExaminationDate(examDate, dateOfBirth, dateOfDeath);
                validator.ValidateExamCodeForDate(existingResults, PkChildExaminationId, ExaminationCode, examDate);
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> ex)
            {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ex.Detail.Title);
                Messenger.Send(error);
                return false;
            }

            CheckAgeWithinScheduleRange(examDate);

            return true;
        }

        public bool ValidateHeight(decimal? height)
        {
            try
            {
                validator.ValidateHeight(height);
                CheckDeviations(height, LookupsUtility.ChildMeasurementType.Height);
                return true;
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> ex)
            {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ex.Detail.Title);
                Messenger.Send(error);
                return false;
            }
        }

        public bool ValidateWeight(decimal? weight)
        {
            try
            {
                validator.ValidateWeight(weight);
                CheckDeviations(weight, LookupsUtility.ChildMeasurementType.Weight);
                return true;
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> ex)
            {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ex.Detail.Title);
                Messenger.Send(error);
                return false;
            }
        }

        public bool ValidateHeadCircumference(decimal headCircumference)
        {   
            try
            {
                validator.ValidateHeadCircumference(headCircumference);
                 return true;
             }
             catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> ex)
             {
                var error = new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle,ex.Detail.Title);
                 Messenger.Send(error);
                 return false;
             }
        }

        public bool ValidateLocationCode(string code)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(code))
            {
                return true;
            }

            GetLocationList();

            if (locationList != null && locationList.All(loc => loc.Code != code))
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(string.Format(InvalidLocationCodeMessage, code), KdpResultsTitle, InvalidLocationCodePrompt));
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        public bool ValidateExaminerCode(string code)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(code))
            {
                return true;
            }

            GetExaminerList();

            if (examinerList == null)
                return true;

            var selectedExaminer = examinerList.FirstOrDefault(ex => ex.Code == code);

            if (selectedExaminer == null)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(string.Format(InvalidHcpCodeMessage, code), KdpResultsTitle, InvalidHcpCodePrompt));
                return false;
            }

            if (selectedExaminer.UsedCode == HLXBoolean.False)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(UnusedExaminerCodeMessage, KdpResultsTitle, UnusedExaminerCodePrompt));
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }
        private void CheckDeviations(decimal? measurementValue, LookupsUtility.ChildMeasurementType measurementType)
        {

            if (ExaminationDate <= DateTime.MinValue || measurementValue == null)
                return;

            var deviationRange = deviationRanges.Value.SingleOrDefault(d => d.MeasurementType == measurementType.ToString());
            var value = (double)measurementValue;

            if (deviationRange.LowerBound < deviationRange.UpperBound && deviationRange.LowerBound < value && value < deviationRange.UpperBound )
                return;

            var warningMessage = string.Format(OutsideExpectedRangeMessage, measurementType.GetDisplayName());
            Messenger.Send(new WarningNotificationMessage(warningMessage, KdpResultsTitle, OutsideExpectedRangePrompt));
        }

        private void CheckBmi(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs eventArgs)
        {
            if (eventArgs.PropertyName == nameof(Bmi) && IsBmiOutsideRange)
            {
                Messenger.Send(
                    new WarningNotificationMessage(
                        string.Format(BmiOutsidePrescribedRangeMessage, BmiLowerBound, BmiUpperBound), 
                        KdpResultsTitle,
                        OutsidePrescribedRangePrompt));
            }
        }

        private void CheckAgeWithinScheduleRange(DateTime examDate)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ExaminationCode)) return;

            var ageInWeeks = dateOfBirth.WeeksBetween(examDate);
            var ageRange = recommendedAgeRangeInWeeks.Value;

            if (ageRange != null && ageRange.Item2 > 0)
            {
                if (ageInWeeks < ageRange.Item1 || ageRange.Item2 < ageInWeeks)
                {
                    {
                        var warningMessage = ageInWeeks < ageRange.Item1? KdpContactGivenBeforeMinAge: KdpContactGivenAfterMaxAge;
                        var warning = new WarningNotificationMessage(string.Format(warningMessage, ExaminationCode),KdpResultsTitle, WarningPrompt);

                        Messenger.Send(warning);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public bool ValidateNumericDescriptorEntered(string input, TYPE_OF_INPUT type)
        {
            int output;
            if (int.TryParse(input, out output))
            {
                List<TypeCodePosition> comparitiveList = type == TYPE_OF_INPUT.EXAMINER_TYPE ? ExaminerTypeCodePositions
                                        : type == TYPE_OF_INPUT.LOCATON_TYPE ? LocationTypeCodePositions
                                        : type == TYPE_OF_INPUT.REASON_NOT_EXAMINED ? ReasonNotExaminedTypeCodePositions
                                        : null;
                
                if(comparitiveList != null && !comparitiveList.Any(c => Convert.ToInt16(c.UiPosition) == output))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                return true;
            }

            return true;
        }

        public List<LookupsSubTO> GetLocationList()
        {
            if (KdpExaminationResultTo.LocationType == null)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(NoLocationTypeMessage, KdpResultsTitle, NoLocationTypePrompt));
                return null;
            }

            try
            {
                 locationList = locationList ?? lookupsService.getLookups(TypeOfLookup.Locations, string.Empty, KdpExaminationResultTo.LocationType.ToString());
                return locationList;
            }
            catch (FaultException ex)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ErrorGettingLocationListPrompt));
                return null;
            }
        }

        public List<LookupsSubTO> GetUsedExaminerList()
        {
            return GetExaminerList()?.Where(ex => ex.UsedCode == HLXBoolean.True).ToList();
        }

        private List<LookupsSubTO> GetExaminerList()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminerCategory))
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(NoExaminerTypeMessage, KdpResultsTitle, NoExaminerTypePrompt));
                return null;
            }

            try
            {
                examinerList = examinerList ?? lookupsService.getLookupsByUsed(TypeOfLookup.Examiners, string.Empty, KdpExaminationResultTo.ExaminerCategory, HLXBoolean.False);
                return examinerList;
            }
            catch (FaultException ex)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, KdpResultsTitle, ErrorGettingExaminerListPrompt));
                return null;
            }
        }

        public int ChangesCount => KdpExaminationResultTo.PropertyChanged.Count;

        private void ClearDataFromHiddenFields()
        {
            if (!Is15Or27MonthContact)
            {
                Development = null;
                DevelopmentFurther = null;
            }

            if (!Is6To8WeekContact)
            {
                Heart = null;
                Hips = null;
                Eyes = null;
                Testes = null;
                PhysicalGeneral = null;
            }

            if (!IsDskContact)
            {
                DskLocomotor = null;
                DskManipulative = null;
                DskVisual = null;
                DskHearingAndLanguage = null;
                DskSpeechAndLanguage = null;
                DskInteractiveSocial = null;
                DskSelfCareSocial = null;
                DskCognitive = null;
            }

            if (IsRskContact) return;
            RskResponsiveParenting = null;
            RskEngagement = null;
            RskSocioFactors = null;
            RskFamilyHealth = null;
            RskFamilySupport = null;
            RskCompleted = null;
        }

        private void ResetDeviations()
        {
            deviationRanges = new Lazy<List<ChildMeasurementsRange>>(() => measurementsService.GetExpectedRanges(gender, dateOfBirth, ExaminationDate));
        }

        private void ResetRecommendedAgeRange()
        {
            recommendedAgeRangeInWeeks = new Lazy<Tuple<double, double>>(() => KdpExaminationService.GetAgeRangeFromExamSchedule(ExaminationCode));
        }

        public void LookUpExamCode()
        {
            var examLookup = new ShowFormLookup(KdpExaminationList);
            Messenger.Send(examLookup);

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(examLookup.Values))
                return;

            ValidateAndSetExamCode(examLookup.Values);
        }

        #region Numeric Descriptor
        private string GetDropDownUiPositionNumber(string typeCode, List<TypeCodePosition> typeCodePositions)
        {
            string uiPostionNumber = typeCodePositions.Where(x => x.TypeCode == typeCode).Select(x => x.UiPosition).FirstOrDefault();

            return uiPostionNumber;
        }

        private void PrependNumericsUiLocationTypeDescriptions()
        {
            foreach (var location in LocationTypeList)
            {
                string uiLocationPositionNumber = GetDropDownUiPositionNumber(location.Code, LocationTypeCodePositions);

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(uiLocationPositionNumber))
                {
                    if (!location.Description.StartsWith(uiLocationPositionNumber))
                        location.Description = string.Format(DropDownCodeDescriptionFormatLocation, uiLocationPositionNumber, location.Description);
                }
            }
        }

        private void PrependNumericsUiExaminerDescriptions()
        {
            foreach (var examiner in ExaminerTypeList)
            {
                string uiExaminerPositionNumber = GetDropDownUiPositionNumber(examiner.Code, ExaminerTypeCodePositions);

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(uiExaminerPositionNumber))
                {
                    if (!examiner.Description.StartsWith(uiExaminerPositionNumber))
                        examiner.Description = string.Format(DropDownCodeDescriptionFormatExaminer, uiExaminerPositionNumber, examiner.Description);
                }
            }
        }

        private void PrependNumericsUiReasonNotExaminedDescriptions()
        {
            foreach (var reasonNotExamined in NotExaminedReasonList)
            {
                string uiReasonNotExaminedPositionNumber = GetDropDownUiPositionNumber(reasonNotExamined.Code, ReasonNotExaminedTypeCodePositions);

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(uiReasonNotExaminedPositionNumber))
                {
                    if (!reasonNotExamined.Description.StartsWith(uiReasonNotExaminedPositionNumber))
                        reasonNotExamined.Description = string.Format(DropDownCodeDescriptionFormatReasonNotExamined, uiReasonNotExaminedPositionNumber, reasonNotExamined.Description);
                }
            }
        }
        #endregion
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.ServiceModel;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using HLX.Net2008.Common;
using Orvex.Business.Validation;
using Orvex.Messages;
using Orvex.Messages.Faults;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;

namespace Orvex.ViewModels
{
    public class KdpScheduledResultDelegate : KdpExaminationResultDelegate
    {
        // ReSharper disable once InconsistentNaming
        private KdpScheduledResultTO scheduledResultTO;
        
        private readonly Dictionary<string, string> genderDictionary = LookupsUtility.Gender(false, LookupsUtility.DICTIONARY_TYPE.STRING);

        public event EventHandler OnFinishedEditing;

        public KdpScheduledResultDelegate(KdpExaminationValidator validator, IMessenger messenger, ILookupService lookupsService, 
            IChildMeasurementsService measurementsService, IKdpExaminationService kdpExaminationService)
            : base(validator, messenger, lookupsService, measurementsService, kdpExaminationService)
        {
        }

        public void Populate(KdpScheduledResultTO kdpExaminationResultTo)
        {
            Populate(kdpExaminationResultTo, kdpExaminationResultTo.Gender, kdpExaminationResultTo.DateOfBirth, kdpExaminationResultTo.DateOfDeath, null, true);

            scheduledResultTO = kdpExaminationResultTo;
        }

        public long ClientId => KdpExaminationResultTo.ClientId;
        public string HsvNumber => scheduledResultTO.HsvNumber;
        public string DateOfBirth => scheduledResultTO.DateOfBirth.ToShortDateString();
        public string Forename => scheduledResultTO.Forename;
        public string Surname => scheduledResultTO.Surname;
        public string Gender => genderDictionary.Keys.SingleOrDefault(k => genderDictionary[k] == scheduledResultTO.Gender);
        public string Address => scheduledResultTO.Address;
        public string NextContactCode { get; set; }
        public string NextContactDescription { get; set; }
        public DateTime? NextContactEarliestDate { get; set; }
        public DateTime? NextContactLatestDate { get; set; }

        public void AssignNextContactDueDetails()
        {
            var nextContactDue = KdpExaminationService.GetNextKdpContactDue(ClientId);
            if (nextContactDue == null) return;
            NextContactCode = nextContactDue.EXAM_CODE;
            NextContactDescription = nextContactDue.EXAM_DESC;
            NextContactEarliestDate = nextContactDue.EARLIEST_DATE_DUE;
            NextContactLatestDate = nextContactDue.LATEST_DATE_DUE;
        }

        public byte Line => scheduledResultTO.Line;

        public string Title => string.Format(Resources.Resources.ScheduledKdpResultsEntryFormTitle, ClientId, Forename, Surname);

        public bool Save()
        {
            try
            {
                SetKdpMeasurementsDeclined();
                scheduledResultTO.ResultEnteredDate = DateTime.Now;
                KdpExaminationService.SaveScheduledResult(scheduledResultTO);
            }
            catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> ex)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, Resources.Resources.ScheduledKdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ExamResultsErrorSavingPrompt));
                return false;
            }
            catch (FaultException<OptimisticLockFault>)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(Resources.Resources.ScheduledExamResultsOptimisticLockMessage, Resources.Resources.ScheduledKdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ExamResultsErrorSavingPrompt));
            }
            catch (FaultException ex)
            {
                Messenger.Send(new NonFatalErrorEncountered(ex.Message, Resources.Resources.ScheduledKdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ExamResultsErrorSavingPrompt));
                return false;
            }

            FinishedEditing();
            return true;
        }

        private void SetKdpMeasurementsDeclined()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(scheduledResultTO.HeightMeasurementDeclined))
            {
                scheduledResultTO.HeightMeasurementDeclined = HLXBoolean.False;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(scheduledResultTO.WeightMeasurementDeclined))
            {
                scheduledResultTO.WeightMeasurementDeclined = HLXBoolean.False;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(scheduledResultTO.HeadMeasurementDeclined))
            {
                scheduledResultTO.HeadMeasurementDeclined = HLXBoolean.False;
            }
        }

        public bool Cancel()
        {
            if (ChangesCount > 0)
            {
                var confirmation = new YesNoConfirmationRequiredMessage(Resources.Resources.ExamResultsConfirmCancelMessage, Resources.Resources.ScheduledKdpResultsTitle, Resources.Resources.ConfirmCancelPrompt);
                Messenger.Send(confirmation);

                if (!confirmation.Response)
                    return false;
            }

            FinishedEditing();
            return true;
        }

        public void FinishedEditing()
        {
            OnFinishedEditing?.Invoke(this, null);
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 7/94: Orvex.ViewModels/KdpScheduledResultDelegate.cs

using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using Orvex.ViewModels.Properties;

namespace Orvex.ViewModels
{
    public abstract class ViewModelBase : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

        [NotifyPropertyChangedInvocator]
        protected virtual void RaisePropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
        protected virtual void RaiseAllPropertyChanged()
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(string.Empty));
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.Runtime.Serialization;
using static Orvex.Resources.Resources;

namespace Orvex.DomainModel
{
    [DataContract]
    public class KdpExamResultModel : BaseModel
    {
        [DataMember]
        public string Sector { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        public long ClientId { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        public long ChildMeasurementId { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        public string ExaminationCode { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        public string ExamDescription { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        public string Contact { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        [Display(Name = nameof(KdpDateOfContact), ResourceType = typeof(Resources.Resources))]
        [DataType(DataType.Date)]
        [DisplayFormat(DataFormatString = "{0:dd/MM/yyyy}", ApplyFormatInEditMode = true)]
        public DateTime ExaminationDate { get; set; }
        
        [DataMember]
        [Display(Name = nameof(OriginLabel), ResourceType = typeof(Resources.Resources))]
        public string OriginDesc { get; set; }
        

        [DataMember]
        [Display(Name = nameof(ReasonNotExaminedLabel), ResourceType = typeof(Resources.Resources))]
        public string ReasonNotExaminedDesc { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        [Display(Name = nameof(KdpConsent), ResourceType = typeof(Resources.Resources))]
        public string Consent { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        [Display(Name = nameof(RescheduleLabel), ResourceType = typeof(Resources.Resources))]
        public string Reschedule { get; set; }

        [DataMember]
        public byte? LocationType { get; set; }

        [DataMember]

>>> FILE 11/94: Orvex.Contracts/HLX.BusinessTier/Common/Model/AbstractTO.cs   [3. Contracts (interfaces, transfer objects, validator, utilities)]

using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Runtime.Serialization;
using System.ServiceModel;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Validator;
using Orvex.Business.Utils;
using Orvex.Messages.Faults;

namespace Orvex.TransferObjects
{
    /**
     * A global TO that all TO's are force to extend to be part of the business model
     * @author HSV
     */
    [DataContract]
    [KnownType("GetKnownTypes")]
    public abstract class AbstractTO : ITO
    {
        /** fires a property change */
        public delegate void FirePropChange(AbstractTO to, Enum key, object value, String prop);

        /** Present a means for prop events to be fired */
        public event FirePropChange propEvent;

        /** An abstract TO to store our data for this TO */
        private Dictionary<Enum, object> datastore;

        /** fires a property change failure event */
        public delegate void FireFailureChange(Enum key, object value, string message, String prop);

        /** Present a means for prop events to be fired */
        public event FireFailureChange propSetFailureEvent;

        /** The id key */
        public enum COMMON_ENUM { ID_PROPERTY };

        /** Stores a list of what properties have changed */
        private ArrayList propChanged;

        private Boolean inlineValidation;

        [DataMember(Order = 0)]
        private readonly Dictionary<string, string> propertyNameDictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();

        private static List<Type> typesToRegisterWithSerializer = new List<Type>();

        /**
        * Prgramatically supply know types. We need to this here as the class type is type parameterised at runtime
        * @return The suitable list of types for serialising/deserialising
        */
        static IEnumerable<Type> GetKnownTypes()
        {
            if (typesToRegisterWithSerializer.Count == 0)
            {
                Type[] types = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetTypes();
                foreach (Type type in types)
                {
                    if (type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AbstractTO)) && !type.IsGenericType)
                    {
                        typesToRegisterWithSerializer.Add(type);
                    }
                }
            }

            return typesToRegisterWithSerializer.ToArray();
        }

        /**
         * performs an equals on the lock parameter and id parameter if one exists. Can be sub classes as necessary to determine equality, although sub classes
         * should always call upon this logic using base.
         * @param obj The object to check
         * @return The equality
         */
        public override bool Equals(Object obj)
        {
            if (obj is AbstractTO)
            {
                Boolean? idequal = null;
                if (Id != null)
                {
                    idequal = Id.Equals(((AbstractTO)obj).Id);
                }

                if (Lock == null)
                {
                    if (idequal != null)
                    {
                        return (bool)idequal;
                    }

                    return base.Equals(obj);
                }
                else
                {
                    if (idequal != null)
                    {
                        return idequal == true && Lock.Equals(((AbstractTO)obj).Lock);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        return Lock.Equals(((AbstractTO)obj).Lock);
                    }
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        /**
         * base the equality on the lock object
         * @return The hash code 
         */
        public override int GetHashCode()
        {
            if (Id != null && Lock != null)
            {
                return Lock.GetHashCode() + Id.GetHashCode();
            }
            else if (Id == null && Lock != null)
            {
                return Lock.GetHashCode();
            }
            else if (Lock == null && Id != null)
            {
                return Id.GetHashCode();
            }

            return base.GetHashCode();
        }

        public AbstractTO()
        {
            propEvent += new FirePropChange(changeProp);
        }

        /**
         * Enbaled invalid validation
         * @return Enabled invalid validation
         */
        [IgnoreDataMember]
        public Boolean EnableInlineValidation
        {
            set
            {
                inlineValidation = value;
            }

            get
            {
                return inlineValidation;
            }
        }

        protected MonitoringList getMonitoringList(AbstractTO rmLst, Enum key, [CallerMemberName]string propertyName = null)
        {
            MonitoringList monitorList = (MonitoringList)getProperty(key);

            if (monitorList == null)
            {
                monitorList = new MonitoringList(new List<AbstractTO>());
                setMonitoringList(monitorList, key, propertyName);
            }

            return monitorList;
        }

        protected MonitoringList<T> GetMonitoringList<T>(Enum key, [CallerMemberName]string propertyName = null)
            where T : AbstractTO
        {
            var monitorList = (MonitoringList<T>)getProperty(key);

            if (monitorList == null)
            {
                monitorList = new MonitoringList<T>();
                setMonitoringList(monitorList, key, propertyName);
            }

            return monitorList;
        }

        [DataMember(Order = 1)]
        public ArrayList PropertyChanged
        {
            get
            {
                if (propChanged == null)
                {
                    propChanged = new ArrayList();
                }

                return propChanged;
            }

            set
            {
                propChanged = value;
            }
        }

        public IEnumerable<string> GetChangedPropertyNames()
        {
            return PropertyChanged
                .Cast<string>()
                .Distinct()
                .Where(pc => propertyNameDictionary.ContainsKey(pc))
                .Select(e => propertyNameDictionary[e]);
        }

        public IEnumerable<string> GetAllPropertyNames()
        {
            return propertyNameDictionary.Values;
        }

        [DataMember(Order = 4)]
        public Boolean EnabledPropChangeEvents { get; set; }

        /**
         * Does the TO contain a changed property of the key specified
         * @param prop The property
         * @return The boolean as to hether this property has changed or not
         */
        public bool hasChanged(Enum prop)
        {
            return PropertyChanged.Contains(prop.ToString());
        }

        public bool HasProperty(Enum prop)
        {
            return Datastore.ContainsKey(prop);
        }

        /** An internal change listener that sets the state of this object */
        private void changeProp(AbstractTO to, Enum key, object value, string method)
        {

            /*This changes are required when AbstractTO contains Monitoring List*/
            //if (to is MonitoringList)
            //{
            //    foreach (AbstractTO _to in (List<AbstractTO>)value)
            //    if (_to.hasChanged())
            //    {
            //        PropertyChanged.Add(key.ToString());
            //    }
            //}
            //else
            //{
            //    /*This was occuring for all the objects*/
            //    PropertyChanged.Add(key.ToString());
            //}

            #region ORIGINAL CODE
            /*This changes are required when AbstractTO contains Monitoring List*/
            if (value is MonitoringList)
            {
                if (((MonitoringList)value).PropertyChanged.Count > 0)
                {
                    PropertyChanged.Add(key.ToString());
                }
            }
            if (!(value is MonitoringList))
            {
                /*This was occuring for all the objects*/
                PropertyChanged.Add(key.ToString());
            }
            #endregion
        }

        /**
        * has changed
        * @return The boolean
        */
        public virtual bool hasChanged()
        {
            return PropertyChanged.Count > 0;
        }

        /**
         * is editable or not
         * @return The boolean
         */
        public bool isEditable()
        {
            return Id != null;
        }

        /** 
         * gets the id
         */
        [DataMember(Order = 2)]
        public String Id
        {
            get
            {

                return (String)getProperty(COMMON_ENUM.ID_PROPERTY);
            }

            set
            {
                setProperty(COMMON_ENUM.ID_PROPERTY, value);
            }
        }

        /** 
         * forces a lock for optimistic checking purposes. Ignore if not interested in optimistic locking
         */
        [DataMember(Order = 3)]
        public String Lock { get; set; }

        [IgnoreDataMember]
        private Dictionary<Enum, object> Datastore
        {
            get { return datastore = datastore ?? new Dictionary<Enum, object>(); }
        }

        /**
         * sets the property value
         * @param key The key to validate
         * @param value The value to validate
         */
        protected void setProperty(Enum key, object value, [CallerMemberName]string propertyName = null)
        {
            PropertyInfo propInfo = null;
            object[] atts = null;
            string interestedProp;

            string enumKey = key.ToString();
            if (propertyNameDictionary.ContainsKey(enumKey))
            {
                interestedProp = propertyNameDictionary[enumKey];
            }
            else
            {
                interestedProp = propertyName;
                propertyNameDictionary.Add(enumKey, interestedProp);
            }

            if (interestedProp != null)
            {
                propInfo = GetType().GetProperty(interestedProp);
            }

            if (propInfo != null)
            {
                atts = propInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(ValidationAttribute), false);
            }

            // fire a validator associated with the property if one exists
            try
            {
                // only fire validator if the prop change events are firing i.e. after everything is initialised
                if (inlineValidation)
                {
                    if (atts != null && atts.Length > 0)
                    {
                        IServiceDataValidator val = ValidatorFactory.Factory.GetValidator(((ValidationAttribute)atts[0]).id);
                        if (val != null)
                        {
                            val.validate(this, key, value, ((ValidationAttribute)atts[0]).message);
                        }
                    }
                }

                // add or update the property key value
                if (!Datastore.ContainsKey(key))
                {
                    Datastore.Add(key, value);
                }
                else
                {
                    Datastore[key] = value;
                }

                // choose whether to fire prop change events
                if (EnabledPropChangeEvents)
                {
                    if (propEvent != null)
                        propEvent.Invoke(this, key, value, interestedProp);
                }

            }

            catch (FaultException<ValidationFault> e)
            {
                ValidationFault ve = e.Detail;
                if (EnabledPropChangeEvents)
                {
                    if (propSetFailureEvent != null)
                        propSetFailureEvent.Invoke(key, value, ve.Message, interestedProp);
                }
                throw e;
            }
        }

        /**
         * setup the listening on the monitoring list
         */
        protected void setMonitoringList(MonitoringList rmLst, Enum key, [CallerMemberName]string propertyName = null)
        {
            String interestedProp = propertyName;
            PropertyInfo propInfo = GetType().GetProperty(interestedProp);

            if (!".ctor".Equals(interestedProp))
            {
                object[] atts = null;
                if (propInfo != null)
                {
                    atts = propInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(ValidationAttribute), false);
                }

                // fire a validator associated with the property if one exists
                if (atts != null && atts.Length > 0)
                {
                    IServiceDataValidator val = ValidatorFactory.Factory.GetValidator(((ValidationAttribute)atts[0]).id);
                    rmLst.SetValidator(val);
                    rmLst.SetContainer(this);
                    rmLst.SetContainedEnum(key);
                }
            }

            setProperty(key, rmLst, interestedProp);
        }

        /**
         * gets the property value
         * @param key The key
         * @return The value to find for the key
         */
        public object getProperty(Enum key)
        {
            object retval = null;
            Datastore.TryGetValue(key, out retval);
            return retval;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets the property from the datastore, if it is not found
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="T"></typeparam>
        /// <param name="key"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public T GetPropertyOrDefault<T>(Enum key)
        {
            if (Datastore.ContainsKey(key))
            {
                return (T)Datastore[key];
            }

            return default(T);
        }

        /** The enumeration that defines the TO. NOTE: All propeties need to be stored using these values */
        public abstract Array getEnumValues();

        /**
         * Generates a top level lock for the object contents
         * @param to The to to install the lock into
         * @return The object with the lock installed         
         */
        public void GenerateLock()
        {
            String emptStr = string.Empty;
            String lck = GenerateLock(this, ref emptStr);
            Lock = lck;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Checks the lock.
        /// Throws an exception if the lock doesn't match.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="other">The other.</param>
        /// <exception cref="FaultException{OptimisticLockFault}"></exception>
        public void CheckLock(AbstractTO other)
        {
            if ((other.Lock == null) || (other.Lock.Trim().Length == 0))
            {
                other.GenerateLock();
            }

            if (!other.Equals(this))
            {
                throw OptimisticLockFault.Default();
            }
        }

        /**
         * Genrates a top level lock for the object contents
         * @param to The to to generate a lock for
         * @param lck The lock string
         * @return The obejct with the lock installed         
         */
        private static String GenerateLock(AbstractTO to, ref String lck)
        {
            Array e = to.getEnumValues();
            to.EnabledPropChangeEvents = true;
            for (int i = 0; i < e.Length; i++)
            {
                object val = to.getProperty((Enum)e.GetValue(i));
                if (val is ICollection)
                {
                    if (((ICollection)val).Count > 0)
                    {
                        IEnumerator num = ((ICollection)val).GetEnumerator();
                        num.MoveNext();
                        if (!(num.Current is AbstractTO))
                        {
                            continue;
                        }
                    }

                    IEnumerator enumerator = ((ICollection)val).GetEnumerator();
                    while (enumerator.MoveNext())
                    {
                        var current = (AbstractTO)enumerator.Current;
                        if (current.EnabledPropChangeEvents != true)
                        {
                            current.GenerateLock();
                        }

                        lck += current.Lock;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    var current = val as AbstractTO;
                    if (current != null)
                    {
                        if (current.EnabledPropChangeEvents != true)
                        {
                            current.GenerateLock();
                        }

                        lck += current.Lock;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (val != null)
                        {
                            lck = lck + val.ToString();
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            return lck;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Resets the change tracking, by clearing PropertyChanged and propertyNameDictionary.
        /// Used prior to sending TO back from service to reduce the serialized data size.
        /// </summary>
        public void ResetChangeTracking()
        {
            PropertyChanged.Clear();
            propertyNameDictionary.Clear();
        }
    }
}
