            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADD3, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string Address4
        {
            get { return (string)getProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADD4); }
            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADD4, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string Address5
        {
            get { return (string)getProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADD5); }
            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADD5, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string Postcode
        {
            get { return (string)getProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.POSTCODE); }
            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.POSTCODE, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string Telephone
        {
            get { return (string)getProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.TELEPHONE); }
            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.TELEPHONE, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string AddressLines
        {
            get { return (string)getProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADDRESS_LINES); }
            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.ADDRESS_LINES, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string UsedCode
        {
            get { return (string)getProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.USED_CODE); }
            set { setProperty(LOOKUPSubTO_ENUM.USED_CODE, value); }
        }

        [DataMember]
        public string ShortDescription { get; set; }

        public string ExaminerType { get; set; }

        public override string ToString()
        {
            return string.Format("{0} - {1}", Code, Description);
        }
    }
}

        /// <param name="examCode">The Exam code t</param>
        /// <returns> Either null if Exam not on schedule, or the Minimum & Maximum age in weeks (or 0 & Double.MaxValue if ages were not prescribed) </returns>

>>> FILE 23/94: Orvex.Contracts/HLX.BusinessTier/Common/Util/DateCalculation.cs   [3. Contracts (interfaces, transfer objects, validator, utilities)]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util

{
    public static class DateCalculation
    {
        public static int DaysBetween(this DateTime dateFrom, DateTime dateTo)
        {
            return (int)(dateTo - dateFrom).TotalDays;
        }
        public static double WeeksBetween(this DateTime dateFrom, DateTime dateTo)
        {
            return (double)dateFrom.DaysBetween(dateTo) / 7;
        }
        public static int MonthsBetween(this DateTime dateFrom, DateTime dateTo)
        {
            return ((dateTo.Year - dateFrom.Year) * 12) - dateFrom.Month + dateTo.Month + (dateTo.Day < dateFrom.Day ? -1 : 0);
        }

        public static int MonthsToDays(int months)
        {
            return (int) (months * 365.25 / 12);
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 23/94: Orvex.Contracts/HLX.BusinessTier/Common/Util/DateCalculation.cs

        /// <summary>
        /// NB. all database values are given as strings at the moment, this may not work for binding int values
        /// </summary>
        public enum DICTIONARY_TYPE
        {
            STRING,
            ENUM
        }
        /*****************************************/
        #region Common Dictionary and ENUM values
        #region YesNoVariations
        #region YES_NO
        public enum YES_NO
        {
            [EnumMember]
            YES,
            [EnumMember]
            NO
        }
        public static Dictionary<String, string> YesNo(bool includeNull, DICTIONARY_TYPE dictionaryType)
        {
            Dictionary<String, string> _dict = new Dictionary<String, string>();
            if (dictionaryType == DICTIONARY_TYPE.ENUM)
            {
                _dict.Add("Yes", YES_NO.YES.ToString());
                _dict.Add("No", YES_NO.NO.ToString());
            }
            else
            {
                _dict.Add("Yes", "Y");
                _dict.Add("No", "N");
            }
            if (includeNull) _dict.Add("", null);
            return _dict;
        }

        #endregion
        #region YES_NO_UNKNOWN
        public enum YES_NO_UNKNOWN
        {
            [EnumMember]
            YES,
            [EnumMember]
            NO,
            [EnumMember]
            UNKNOWN
        }
        public static Dictionary<String, string> YesNoUnknown(bool includeNull, DICTIONARY_TYPE dictionaryType, bool useNotKnown = false)
        {
            Dictionary<String, string> _dict = new Dictionary<String, string>();
            if (dictionaryType == DICTIONARY_TYPE.ENUM)
            {
                _dict.Add("Yes", YES_NO_UNKNOWN.YES.ToString());
                _dict.Add("No", YES_NO_UNKNOWN.NO.ToString());

                _dict.Add(!useNotKnown ? "Unknown" : "Not Known", YES_NO_UNKNOWN.UNKNOWN.ToString());
            }
            else
            {
                _dict.Add("Yes", "Y");
                _dict.Add("No", "N");
                _dict.Add(!useNotKnown ? "Unknown" : "Not Known", "U");
            }
            if (includeNull) _dict.Add("", null);
            return _dict;
        }

        #endregion
        #endregion
        #region Date Estimated

            NO

                dict.Add("Month", DATE_ESTIMATED.MONTH.ToString());
                if (includeNo)
                    dict.Add("No", DATE_ESTIMATED.NO.ToString());
            }
            else
            {
                dict.Add("Day", "D");
                dict.Add("Month", "M");
                if (includeNo)
                    dict.Add("No", "N");
            }
            if (includeNull) dict.Add("", null);
            return dict;

        public enum IMMUNISATION_STATUS
        {
            [EnumMember]
            [Display(Name = nameof(Resources.TreatmentCentre), ResourceType = typeof(Resources))]

            [EnumMember]
            [Display(Name = nameof(Resources.School), ResourceType = typeof(Resources))]

            [EnumMember]
            [Display(Name = nameof(Resources.OutsideDistrict), ResourceType = typeof(Resources))]
            OUTSIDE_DISTRICT = 0
        }
        public static Dictionary<String, string> ImmunisationStatus(bool includeNull, DICTIONARY_TYPE dictionaryType, bool defaultsOnly = false)
        {
            // Note: defaultsOnly is used for School Authority Options where the default immunisation status can be set.
            // "Outside District" is a valid option for an individual child, but is not valid as a default value.

            var dict = new Dictionary<String, string>();
            if (dictionaryType == DICTIONARY_TYPE.ENUM)
            {
                dict.Add("Treatment Centre", IMMUNISATION_STATUS.TREATMENT_CENTRE.ToString());
                dict.Add("School", IMMUNISATION_STATUS.SCHOOL.ToString());
                if (!defaultsOnly)
                    dict.Add("Outside District", IMMUNISATION_STATUS.OUTSIDE_DISTRICT.ToString());
            }
            else
            {
                dict.Add("Treatment Centre", "3");
                dict.Add("School", "1");
                if (!defaultsOnly)
                    dict.Add("Outside District", "0");
            }
            if (includeNull) dict.Add("", null);
            return dict;
        }

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util
{
    public static class StringExtensions
    {
        public static string ToTitleCase(this string val)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(val)) return "";

            return CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.TextInfo.ToTitleCase(val);
        }

        public static string CamelToUpperUnderscored(this string name)
        {
            var result = Regex.Replace(name, "[a-z][A-Z]", m => m.Value[0] + "_" + m.Value[1]);

            return result.ToUpper();
        }
        public static string CamelToSpacedText(this string name)
        {
            var result = Regex.Replace(name, "[a-z][A-Z]", m => m.Value[0] + " " + m.Value[1]);

            return result;
        }
        public static string StripPunctuation(this string s)
        {
            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            foreach(char c in s)
            {
                if(!char.IsPunctuation(c)) sb.Append(c);
            }
            return sb.ToString();
        }


        public static string LowerCaseSentenceToCamelCaseSentence(this string name)
        {


            string result = Regex.Replace(name, @"\b[a-z]", m => m.ToString().ToUpper(), RegexOptions.None);
            result = Regex.Replace(result, @"(?<=\b[A-Z])\w+", m => m.ToString().ToLower(), RegexOptions.None);

            return result;

        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Method returns input string ,but returns "" if string is null or empty
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="input"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public static string SafeNotNullValue(this string input)
        {

            if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
            {
                return "";
            }
            return input;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Function returns false if string is null or whitespace
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="input"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public static bool StringTextExists(this string input)
        {

            return !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input);
        }

        public static string TrimToMaxLength(this string input, int max)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return "";
            if (max == 0) max = input.Length;
            return input.Length > max ? input.Substring(0, max) : input ;
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 26/94: Orvex.Contracts/HLX.BusinessTier/Common/Util/UnitOfWorkTransferObjectExtensions.cs   [3. Contracts (interfaces, transfer objects, validator, utilities)]

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Linq;
using System.Linq.Expressions;
using System.Reflection;
using AutoMapper;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using OrvexBusinessTier.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using LinqKit;

namespace HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util
{
    /// <summary>
    /// An extension class to support persisting Transfer Objects and monitoring lists to a unit of work / repository.
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
    public static class UnitOfWorkTransferObjectExtensions
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<Tuple<Type, Type>, object> KeySelectors = new Dictionary<Tuple<Type, Type>, object>();

        #region Public Static Members

        /// <summary>
        /// Checks the lock for each of the changed objects in the monitoring list.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="list">The monitoring list.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The query returning the current data.</param>
        /// <param name="useTracking">if set to <c>true</c> track the results in the database context.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>The latest model data for the changed items, so that latest timestamps etc can be retrieved.</returns>
        public static IList<TModel> CheckLock<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                       MonitoringList<TTransferObject> list,
                                                                       IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                       bool useTracking = false)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var changedData = list.WhereChanged();
            return uow.CheckLockAndUpdate(changedData, currentDataQuery, checkLock: true, update: false, useTracking: useTracking);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Checks the lock for the changed transfer object.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObject">The transfer object.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The query returning the current data.</param>
        /// <param name="useTracking">if set to <c>true</c> track the results in the database context.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>The latest model data for the item, so that latest timestamps etc can be retrieved.</returns>
        public static TModel CheckLock<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                TTransferObject transferObject,
                                                                IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                bool useTracking = false)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var changedData = (new List<TTransferObject> { transferObject }).AsQueryable();
            return uow.CheckLockAndUpdate(changedData, currentDataQuery, checkLock: true, update: false, useTracking: useTracking).Single();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Checks the lock for each of the changed objects changed data list.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="changedData">A query returning the changed transfer objects.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The query returning the current data.</param>
        /// <param name="useTracking">if set to <c>true</c> track the results in the database context.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>The latest model data for the changed items, so that latest timestamps etc can be retrieved.</returns>
        public static IList<TModel> CheckLock<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                       IQueryable<TTransferObject> changedData,
                                                                       IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                       bool useTracking = false)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            return uow.CheckLockAndUpdate(changedData, currentDataQuery, checkLock: true, update: false, useTracking: useTracking);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Deletes the specified Transfer Object from the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObject">The transfer object to delete.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        public static void DeleteTransferObject<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow, TTransferObject transferObject)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var transferObjects = (new List<TTransferObject> { transferObject }).AsQueryable();
            uow.DeleteTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(transferObjects);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Deletes the specified list of Transfer Objects from the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObjects">The list of transfer objects to delete.</param>
        /// <param name="useLocal">if set to <c>true</c> use the Local cache instead of mapping TOs into models to be deleted.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        public static void DeleteTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow, IQueryable<TTransferObject> transferObjects, bool useLocal = false)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            try
            {
                var repository = uow.Repository<TModel>();
                List<TModel> models = null;

                if (useLocal)
                {
                    var keySelector = GetKeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>();
                    models = repository.Local().Join(transferObjects, keySelector.ModelKeySelector.Compile(), keySelector.TransferObjectKeySelector.Compile(),
                        (entity, to) => entity).ToList();

                    if (models.Count != transferObjects.Count())
                    {
                        models = null;
                    }
                }

                if (models == null)
                {
                    models = Mapper.Map<List<TModel>>(transferObjects, opt => { opt.Items["uow"] = uow; opt.Items["CascadeDelete"] = true; });
                }

                foreach (var model in models)
                {
                    repository.Delete(model);
                }
            }
            catch (System.Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Inserts the specified Transfer Object in the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObject">The transfer object to insert.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>The re-retrieved inserted model (so that caller can access new ID fields etc).</returns>
        public static TModel InsertTransferObject<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow, TTransferObject transferObject)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var transferObjects = (new List<TTransferObject> { transferObject }).AsQueryable();
            return uow.InsertTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(transferObjects).Single();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Inserts the specified list of Transfer Objects in the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObjects">The list of transfer objects to insert.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>A list of the re-retrieved inserted models (so that caller can access new ID fields etc).</returns>
        public static IList<TModel> InsertTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow, IQueryable<TTransferObject> transferObjects)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            try
            {
                var repository = uow.Repository<TModel>();
                var models = Mapper.Map<List<TModel>>(transferObjects, opt => opt.Items["uow"] = uow);
                return models.Select(repository.Insert).ToList();
            }
            catch (System.Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Save a monitoring list in the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// Returns the updated models.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="list">The monitoring list to persist.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The current data query.</param>
        /// <param name="checkLock">if set to <c>true</c> check the lock.</param>
        /// <returns>A list of the re-retrieved updated and inserted models (so that caller can access new ID fields etc).</returns>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        public static IList<TModel> SaveMonitoringList<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                                MonitoringList<TTransferObject> list,
                                                                                IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                                bool checkLock = true)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            return uow.SaveMonitoringListInternalUseOnly(list, currentDataQuery, checkLock, includeAllExisting: false);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Save the monitoring list in the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// By default it returns ALL models regardless of whether or not they were updated.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="list">The monitoring list to persist.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The current data query.</param>
        /// <param name="checkLock">if set to <c>true</c> check the lock.</param>
        /// <param name="includeAllExisting">If set to <c>true</c> process and update all models, not just edited ones.</param>
        /// <returns>A list of ALL of the re-retrieved models (so that caller can access new ID fields etc).</returns>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        [EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
        public static IList<TModel> SaveMonitoringListInternalUseOnly<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                                MonitoringList<TTransferObject> list,
                                                                                IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                                bool checkLock = true,
                                                                                bool includeAllExisting = true)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var useLocal = currentDataQuery == null;
            uow.DeleteTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(list.RemovedTO.Cast<TTransferObject>().AsQueryable(), useLocal);

            var modelsToUpdate = includeAllExisting ? list.ExistingTO : list.GetUpdated<AbstractTO>().ToList();
            var updatedModels = uow.UpdateTransferObjects(modelsToUpdate.Cast<TTransferObject>().AsQueryable(), currentDataQuery, checkLock);

            var insertedModels = uow.InsertTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(list.AddedTO.Cast<TTransferObject>().AsQueryable());

            return insertedModels.Concat(updatedModels).ToList();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Update the specified Transfer Object in the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObject">The Transfer Object to update.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The current data query.</param>
        /// <param name="checkLock">if set to <c>true</c> check the lock.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>The updated model (so that caller can access new ID fields etc).</returns>
        public static TModel UpdateTransferObject<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                           TTransferObject transferObject,
                                                                           IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                           bool checkLock = true)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            if (!TOUtil.hasChanged(transferObject))
            {
                return null;
            }

            var changedData = (new List<TTransferObject> { transferObject }).AsQueryable();
            return uow.CheckLockAndUpdate(changedData, currentDataQuery, checkLock, update: true, useTracking: true).Single();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Update the specified list of Transfer Objects in the appropriate repository in the specified unit of work.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="changedData">The list of transfer objects to update.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The current data query.</param>
        /// <param name="checkLock">if set to <c>true</c> check the lock.</param>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>A list of the updated models (so that caller can access new ID fields etc).</returns>
        public static IList<TModel> UpdateTransferObjects<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                                   IQueryable<TTransferObject> changedData,
                                                                                   IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                                   bool checkLock = true)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            return uow.CheckLockAndUpdate(changedData, currentDataQuery, checkLock, update: true, useTracking: true);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets the monitoring list changes as an IQueryable.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="list">The list.</param>
        /// <returns>An IQueryable list of the changed Transfer Objects.</returns>
        public static IQueryable<TTransferObject> WhereChanged<TTransferObject>(this MonitoringList<TTransferObject> list)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            return list.ExistingTO.Where(TOUtil.hasChanged).Cast<TTransferObject>().AsQueryable();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Converts the list of models into transfer objects and returns as a MonitoringList.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="entities">The entities.</param>
        /// <remarks>NB there is no reference here to the type of the transfer object as this is done during CreateMap.
        /// Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>A monitoring list of transfer objects.</returns>
        public static MonitoringList<TTransferObject> ToMonitoringList<TTransferObject>(this IQueryable<object> entities)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            try
            {
                return Mapper.Map<MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>(entities);
            }
            catch (System.Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Converts the list of models into transfer objects and returns as a MonitoringList.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel"></typeparam>
        /// <param name="entities">The entities.</param>
        /// <remarks>NB there is no reference here to the type of the transfer object as this is done during CreateMap.
        /// Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        /// <returns>A monitoring list of transfer objects.</returns>
        public static MonitoringList<TTransferObject> ToMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>(this ICollection<TModel> entities)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            try
            {
                return Mapper.Map<MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>(entities);
            }
            catch (System.Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps the specified entity into an instance of the specified TransferObject type.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="entity">The entity.</param>
        /// <returns>The transfer object.</returns>
        public static TTransferObject To<TTransferObject>(this object entity)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            return Mapper.Map<TTransferObject>(entity);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Sets the key selector for the given types.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="keySelector">The key selector.</param>
        public static void SetKeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>(KeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel> keySelector)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var key = new Tuple<Type, Type>(typeof(TTransferObject), typeof(TModel));

            if (KeySelectors.ContainsKey(key))
            {
                return;
            }

            KeySelectors.Add(key, keySelector);
        }

        #endregion

        #region Private Static Members

        /// <summary>
        /// Creates a list of transfer objects \ model pairs and checks their lock and updates them if the caller requires.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The uow.</param>
        /// <param name="changedData">The changed data.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The current data query.</param>
        /// <param name="checkLock">if set to <c>true</c> check the lock.</param>
        /// <param name="update">if set to <c>true</c> update the database.</param>
        /// <param name="useTracking">if set to <c>true</c> track the results in the database context.</param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        private static IList<TModel> CheckLockAndUpdate<TTransferObject, TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                                 IQueryable<TTransferObject> changedData,
                                                                                 IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery = null,
                                                                                 bool checkLock = true,
                                                                                 bool update = true,
                                                                                 bool useTracking = false)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            if (currentDataQuery == null)
            {
                currentDataQuery = uow.Repository<TModel>().Local();
            }
            else
            {
                if (!useTracking)
                {
                    currentDataQuery = uow.Repository<TModel>().AsNoTracking(currentDataQuery);
                }
            }

            var currentModels = GetCurrentModels(changedData, currentDataQuery);
            return CheckLockAndUpdate(uow, currentModels, checkLock, update).ToList();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Iterates through the given list of transfer objects \ model pairs and optionally checks each objects lock.
        /// Optionally maps the transfer object into the model and updates it.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The unit of work.</param>
        /// <param name="modelPairs">The model pairs.</param>
        /// <param name="checkLock">if set to <c>true</c> check the lock.</param>
        /// <param name="update">if set to <c>true</c> update the database.</param>
        /// <returns>
        /// The latest model data for the changed items, so that latest timestamps etc can be retrieved.
        /// </returns>
        private static IEnumerable<TModel> CheckLockAndUpdate<TTransferObject, TModel>(IUnitOfWork uow,
                                                                                       IEnumerable<Tuple<TTransferObject, TModel>> modelPairs,
                                                                                       bool checkLock,
                                                                                       bool update)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            foreach (var pair in modelPairs)
            {
                if (!TOUtil.hasChanged(pair.Item1))
                {
                    yield return pair.Item2;
                    continue;
                }

                if (checkLock)
                {
                    pair.Item1.CheckLock(Mapper.Map<TTransferObject>(pair.Item2));
                }

                if (update)
                {
                    var editedModel = Mapper.Map(pair.Item1, pair.Item2, opt =>
                    {
                        opt.Items["uow"] = uow;
                        opt.Items["checkLock"] = checkLock;
                    });
                    yield return UpdateModel(uow, pair.Item1, editedModel);
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets the current models.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="changedData">The changed data.</param>
        /// <param name="currentDataQuery">The current data query.</param>
        /// <returns>A list of current models paired with the associated transfer object.</returns>
        private static IEnumerable<Tuple<TTransferObject, TModel>> GetCurrentModels<TTransferObject, TModel>(IQueryable<TTransferObject> changedData,
                                                                                                             IQueryable<TModel> currentDataQuery)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var keySelector = GetKeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>();

            var keyMatchExpression = keySelector.CreateContainsExpression(changedData);

            var currentData = currentDataQuery.AsExpandable()
                .Where(keyMatchExpression)
                .ToList();

            var toPairs = currentData.Join(changedData, keySelector.ModelKeySelector.Compile(), keySelector.TransferObjectKeySelector.Compile(),
                (entity, to) => new Tuple<TTransferObject, TModel>(to, entity));

            return toPairs;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets the changed model properties.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="transferObject">The transfer object.</param>
        /// <returns>An array of the names of the changed properties.</returns>
        private static string[] GetChangedModelProperties<TTransferObject, TModel>(TTransferObject transferObject)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var mappedPropertyNames = GetMappedPropertyNames<TTransferObject, TModel>();
            var changedModelProperties = transferObject
                .GetChangedPropertyNames()
                .Where(mappedPropertyNames.ContainsKey)
                .Select(p => mappedPropertyNames[p]).ToArray();
            return changedModelProperties;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets the key selector for the types.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <returns></returns>
        /// <exception cref="System.InvalidOperationException">Key selector not defined</exception>
        private static KeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel> GetKeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>()
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var key = new Tuple<Type, Type>(typeof(TTransferObject), typeof(TModel));

            if (!KeySelectors.ContainsKey(key))
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Key selector not defined");
            }

            return (KeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>)KeySelectors[key];
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets a dictionary of the property name maps between the specified TransferObject type and Model type.
        /// Keyed on Transfer Object property name.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the models persisted in the repository.</typeparam>
        /// <returns>A dictionary of property maps.</returns>
        /// <remarks>Relies upon AutoMapper being already initialised.</remarks>
        private static Dictionary<string, string> GetMappedPropertyNames<TTransferObject, TModel>()
        {
            Func<PropertyMap, bool> isValidMap = m => !m.IsIgnored() && m.SourceMember != null && m.DestinationProperty != null;

            TypeMap map = Mapper.FindTypeMapFor<TTransferObject, TModel>();
            var properties = map.GetPropertyMaps()
                .Where(isValidMap)
                .ToDictionary(p => p.SourceMember.Name, p => p.DestinationProperty.Name);

            //Note this helps with an AutoMapper issue where mappings for m.SourceMember are null when the SourceMember is boolean,
            //this means mapping to string (Y/N) from boolean get lost, but the as long as reverse mapping is defined it will still get picked up 
            //cref: https://github.com/AutoMapper/AutoMapper/issues/898
            TypeMap mapReverse = Mapper.FindTypeMapFor<TModel, TTransferObject>();
            mapReverse.GetPropertyMaps()
                .Where(isValidMap)
                .Where(m => !properties.ContainsKey(m.DestinationProperty.Name))
                .ForEach(p => properties.Add(p.DestinationProperty.Name, p.SourceMember.Name));

            return properties;
        }
        
        /// <summary>
        /// Updates the specified model using the changed properties list from the transfer object.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="uow">The uow.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObject">The transfer object.</param>
        /// <param name="model">The model.</param>
        /// <returns>The updated model.</returns>
        private static TModel UpdateModel<TTransferObject, TModel>(IUnitOfWork uow, TTransferObject transferObject, TModel model)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var changedModelProperties = GetChangedModelProperties<TTransferObject, TModel>(transferObject);
            var repository = uow.Repository<TModel>();
            return repository.Update(model, changedModelProperties);
        }

        #endregion

        public class KeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            private readonly bool isComplex;
            private readonly Expression<Func<TTransferObject, object>> transferObjectKeySelector;
            private readonly Expression<Func<TModel, object>> modelKeySelector;

            /// <summary>
            /// Initializes a new instance of the <see cref="KeySelector{TTransferObject, TModel}"/> class.
            /// </summary>
            /// <param name="transferObjectKeySelector">The transfer object key selector.</param>
            /// <param name="modelKeySelector">The model key selector.</param>
            public KeySelector(Expression<Func<TTransferObject, object>> transferObjectKeySelector,
                               Expression<Func<TModel, object>> modelKeySelector)
            {
                this.modelKeySelector = modelKeySelector;
                this.transferObjectKeySelector = transferObjectKeySelector;
                isComplex = modelKeySelector.Body is NewExpression;
            }

            /// <summary>
            /// Gets the model key selector.
            /// </summary>
            /// <value>
            /// The model key selector.
            /// </value>
            public Expression<Func<TModel, object>> ModelKeySelector
            {
                get { return modelKeySelector; }
            }

            /// <summary>
            /// Gets the transfer object key selector.
            /// </summary>
            /// <value>
            /// The transfer object key selector.
            /// </value>
            public Expression<Func<TTransferObject, object>> TransferObjectKeySelector
            {
                get { return transferObjectKeySelector; }
            }

            public bool IsComplex
            {
                get { return isComplex; }
            }

            /// <summary>
            /// Creates an expression which mimics the 'contains' functionality found in Linq to Sql but missing from Linq to Entity.
            /// Takes the specified list of transfer objects and generates
            /// a concatenated Or expression which will test an instance of the model to see if it's in the list.
            /// </summary>
            /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
            /// <param name="transferObjects">The list of transfer objects to match against.</param>
            /// <returns>A 'contains' expression.</returns>
            public Expression<Func<TModel, bool>> CreateContainsExpression(IQueryable<TTransferObject> transferObjects)
            {
                var modelPar = Expression.Parameter(typeof(TModel));

                var matcherExpression = GetKeyMatcherExpression(modelPar);

                Expression body = Expression.Constant(false);

                foreach (var to in transferObjects)
                {
                    body = Expression.OrElse(body, Expression.Invoke(matcherExpression, Expression.Constant(to)));
                }

                var lambda = Expression.Lambda<Func<TModel, bool>>(body, modelPar);

                return lambda;
            }

            /// <summary>
            /// Creates an expression which will check accept a transfer object and compare its keys with a Model object.
            /// </summary>
            /// <param name="modelPar">The model parameter.</param>
            /// <returns>A lambda which returns true if a match is found.</returns>
            private Expression<Func<TTransferObject, bool>> GetKeyMatcherExpression(ParameterExpression modelPar)
            {
                var toPar = Expression.Parameter(typeof(TTransferObject));

                var expression = GetPropertyInfos()
                    .Select(pis => Expression.Equal(Expression.Property(modelPar, pis.Item2), Expression.Property(toPar, pis.Item1)))
                    .Aggregate(Expression.AndAlso);

                var lambda = Expression.Lambda<Func<TTransferObject, bool>>(expression, toPar);

                return lambda;
            }

            /// <summary>
            /// Gets the property information for a given Member expression.
            /// </summary>
            /// <typeparam name="T">The expression type (probably a Func of model returning a key type) </typeparam>
            /// <param name="keySelector">The key selector.</param>
            /// <returns>The PropertyInfo.</returns>
            private static PropertyInfo GetPropertyInfo<T>(Expression<T> keySelector)
            {
                MemberExpression body = (keySelector.Body is UnaryExpression)
                    ? (MemberExpression)((UnaryExpression)keySelector.Body).Operand
                    : (MemberExpression)keySelector.Body;
                var propertyInfo = (PropertyInfo)body.Member;
                return propertyInfo;
            }

            /// <summary>
            /// Gets the property infos from the key expressions.
            /// </summary>
            /// <returns></returns>
            private IEnumerable<Tuple<PropertyInfo, PropertyInfo>> GetPropertyInfos()
            {
                if (isComplex)
                {
                    var modelBody = (modelKeySelector.Body as NewExpression);
                    Debug.Assert(modelBody != null);
                    var toBody = (transferObjectKeySelector.Body as NewExpression);
                    Debug.Assert(toBody != null);

                    for (int i = 0; i < modelBody.Arguments.Count; i++)
                    {
                        var arg1 = (MemberExpression)toBody.Arguments[i];
                        var arg2 = (MemberExpression)modelBody.Arguments[i];
                        var toSelector = (PropertyInfo)arg1.Member;
                        var modelSelector = (PropertyInfo)arg2.Member;

                        yield return new Tuple<PropertyInfo, PropertyInfo>(toSelector, modelSelector);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    yield return new Tuple<PropertyInfo, PropertyInfo>(GetPropertyInfo(transferObjectKeySelector), GetPropertyInfo(modelKeySelector));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 27/94: Orvex.Contracts/HLX.BusinessTier/Common/Validator/BusinessValidator/Examinations/KdpExaminationValidator.cs   [3. Contracts (interfaces, transfer objects, validator, utilities)]

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using Orvex.Messages.Faults;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using static Orvex.Resources.Resources;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace Orvex.Business.Validation
{
    public class KdpExaminationValidator
    {
        private const string YesValue = "Y";

        private const decimal HeightMinValue = 0;
        private const decimal WeightMinValue = 0;
        private const decimal HeadCircumferenceMinValue = 0;

        private const decimal HeightMaxValue = 1000;
        private const decimal WeightMaxValue = 1000;
        private const decimal HeadCircumferenceMaxValue = 1000;

        private readonly string kdpInvalidHeightMessage = string.Format(InvalidHeightMessage, HeightMinValue, HeightMaxValue);
        private readonly string kdpInvalidWeightMessage = string.Format(InvalidWeightMessage, WeightMinValue, WeightMaxValue);
        private readonly string kdpInvalidHeadCircumferenceMessage = string.Format(InvalidHeadCircumferenceMessage, HeadCircumferenceMinValue, HeadCircumferenceMaxValue);
        


        public void ValidateNewExamCode(string examCode, List<LookupsSubTO> validExaminations)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(examCode))
                return;

            if (validExaminations.All(ex => ex.Code != examCode))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(KdpInvalidExamCodeMessage, InvalidExamCodePrompt);
            }
        }

        public void ValidateExamCodeForDuplicates(List<KdpExaminationResultTO> existingResults, string examCode, long resultId)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(examCode))
                return;

            if (existingResults.Any(r => r.ExaminationCode == examCode && r.PkChildExaminationId != resultId))
            {
                throw ValidationWarningFault.Default(KdpDuplicateExamCodeMessage, DuplicateExamCodePrompt);
            }
        }

        public void ValidateExamCodeForDate(List<KdpExaminationResultTO> existingResults, long id, string examCode, DateTime examDate)
        {
            if (ResultAlreadyExistsForCodeAndDate(existingResults, id, examCode, examDate))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(KdpResultAlreadyEnteredMessage, DuplicateExamCodePrompt);
            }
        }

        private static bool ResultAlreadyExistsForCodeAndDate(List<KdpExaminationResultTO> existingResults, long id, string examCode, DateTime examDate)
        {
            if (existingResults == null)
                return false;

            return existingResults.Any(r => r.PkChildExaminationId != id && r.ExaminationCode == examCode && r.ExaminationDate.Date == examDate.Date);
        }

        public void ValidateKdpConsent(long clientId, bool hasConsent)
        {
            if (!hasConsent)
            {
                throw ValidationWarningFault.Default(string.Format(NoConsentMessage, clientId), ChildHasNoConsentPrompt);
            }
        }

        public void ValidateExaminationDate(DateTime enteredDate, DateTime dateOfBirth, DateTime? dateOfDeath)
        {
            if (IsExaminationDateInTheFuture(enteredDate))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(FutureExamDateMessage, InvalidExamDatePrompt);
            }

            if (!IsExaminationDateAfterBirthDate(enteredDate, dateOfBirth))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(ExamDateBeforeDobMessage, InvalidExamDatePrompt);
            }

            if (!IsExaminationDateBeforeDeathDate(enteredDate, dateOfDeath))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(ExamDateAfterDeathMessage, InvalidExamDatePrompt);
            }
        }

        private static bool IsExaminationDateInTheFuture(DateTime enteredDate)
        {
            return enteredDate.Date > DateTime.Today;
        }

        private static bool IsExaminationDateAfterBirthDate(DateTime enteredDate, DateTime dateOfBirth)
        {
            return enteredDate >= dateOfBirth;
        }

        private static bool IsExaminationDateBeforeDeathDate(DateTime enteredDate, DateTime? dateOfDeath)
        {
            return !dateOfDeath.HasValue || enteredDate <= dateOfDeath;
        }

        public void ValidateHeight(decimal? height)
        {
            if (!ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(height, HeightMinValue, HeightMaxValue))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(kdpInvalidHeightMessage, InvalidHeightPrompt);
            }
        }

        public void ValidateWeight(decimal? weight)
        {
            if (!ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(weight, WeightMinValue, WeightMaxValue))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(kdpInvalidWeightMessage, InvalidWeightPrompt);
            }
        }

        public void ValidateHeadCircumference(decimal headCircumference)
        {
            if (headCircumference <= 0)
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(HeadCircumferenceMinusNumberErrorMessage, InvalidHeadCircumferencePrompt);
            }
            if (headCircumference >= 100)
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(HeadCircumferenceInvalidFormatMessage, InvalidHeadCircumferencePrompt);

            }
            if (!ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(headCircumference, HeadCircumferenceMinValue, HeadCircumferenceMaxValue))
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(kdpInvalidHeadCircumferenceMessage, InvalidHeadCircumferencePrompt);
            }
        }

        private static bool ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(decimal? nullableValue, decimal minValue, decimal maxValue)
        {
            return !nullableValue.HasValue || (nullableValue.Value > minValue && nullableValue.Value < maxValue);
        }

        private static bool NoResults(KdpExaminationResultTO results)
        {
            return results.Height == null &&
                   results.Weight == null &&
                   results.HeadCircumference == null &&
                   results.DomesticViolenceConcern == null &&
                   results.RskResponsiveParenting == null &&
                   results.RskEngagement == null &&
                   results.RskSocioFactors == null &&
                   results.RskFamilyHealth == null &&
                   results.RskFamilySupport == null &&
                   results.Development == null &&
                   results.EvidenceOfXgm == null &&
                   results.Hips == null &&
                   results.Heart == null &&
                   results.Eyes == null &&
                   results.Testes == null &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskCognitive) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskHearingAndLanguage) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskInteractiveSocial) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskLocomotor) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskManipulative) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskSelfCareSocial) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskSpeechAndLanguage) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DskVisual) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.ReceivingBoostSchemeService) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.LookedAfterChild) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.OnChildProtectionRegister) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.OnwardReferralsMade) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.RskCompleted) &&
                   string.IsNullOrEmpty(results.DevelopmentFurther);
        }

        public void ValidateOnSave(KdpExaminationResultTO newExaminationResult, DateTime dateOfBirth, DateTime? dateOfDeath, List<KdpExaminationResultTO> existingResults = null)
        {
            var errorMessage = new StringBuilder();

            byte count = 1;

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(newExaminationResult.ExaminationCode))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {NoExamCodeMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }

            if (newExaminationResult.ExaminationDate == DateTime.MinValue)
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {NoExamDateMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }
            else if (IsExaminationDateInTheFuture(newExaminationResult.ExaminationDate))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {FutureExamDateMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }
            else if (!IsExaminationDateAfterBirthDate(newExaminationResult.ExaminationDate, dateOfBirth))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {ExamDateBeforeDobMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }
            else if (!IsExaminationDateBeforeDeathDate(newExaminationResult.ExaminationDate, dateOfDeath))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {ExamDateAfterDeathMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }

            if (!ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(newExaminationResult.Height, HeightMinValue, HeightMaxValue))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {kdpInvalidHeightMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }

            if (!ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(newExaminationResult.Weight, WeightMinValue, WeightMaxValue))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {kdpInvalidWeightMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }

            if (!ValueIsNullOrWithinRange(newExaminationResult.HeadCircumference, HeadCircumferenceMinValue, HeadCircumferenceMaxValue))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {kdpInvalidHeadCircumferenceMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(newExaminationResult.ReasonNotExamined))
            {
                if (newExaminationResult.Reschedule == YesValue)
                {
                    errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {RescheduleOnlyIfNotExaminedMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
                }

                if (NoResults(newExaminationResult))
                {
                    errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {NoResultsEnteredMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (!NoResults(newExaminationResult))
                {
                    errorMessage.Append($"{count++}: {ResultsRecordedButNotExamined}{Environment.NewLine}");
                }
            }

            if (ResultAlreadyExistsForCodeAndDate(existingResults, newExaminationResult.PkChildExaminationId, newExaminationResult.ExaminationCode, newExaminationResult.ExaminationDate))
            {
                errorMessage.Append($"{count}: {KdpResultAlreadyEnteredMessage}{Environment.NewLine}");
            }

            if (errorMessage.Length > 0)
            {
                throw ValidationFault.Default(errorMessage.ToString(), ValidationErrorsPrompt);
            }
        }
    }
}

namespace HLX.Net2008.Common
{
    public static class HLXBoolean
    {
        public const string True = "Y";
        public const string False = "N";

        public static bool HLXBooleanToBoolean(string hlxBoolean)
        {
            return hlxBoolean == True;
        }

        public static string BoolToChar(bool booleanValue)
        {
            return booleanValue ? True : False;
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 29/94: Orvex.Contracts/IMessenger.cs   [3. Contracts (interfaces, transfer objects, validator, utilities)]

using System;

namespace Orvex.Services.Internal
{

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Linq.Expressions;
using AutoMapper;
using AutoMapper.Impl;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig.Resolvers;

namespace OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig
{
    public static class AutoMapperExtensions
    {
        public static IMappingExpression<TTransferObject, TModel> MapList<TModel, TTransferObject>(this IMappingExpression<TTransferObject, TModel> mapper)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            Mapper.CreateMap<MonitoringList<TTransferObject>, List<TModel>>()
                  .ConvertUsing(ml => Mapper.Map<List<TTransferObject>, List<TModel>>(ml.GetAddedAndExisting<TTransferObject>()));
            return mapper;
        }
        public static IMappingExpression<TSource, TDestination> IgnoreUnMapped<TSource, TDestination>(this IMappingExpression<TSource, TDestination> expression)
        {
            TypeMap map = Mapper.FindTypeMapFor<TSource, TDestination>();

            foreach (var property in map.GetUnmappedPropertyNames())
            {
                expression.ForMember(property, opt => opt.Ignore());
            }

            return expression;
        }

        public static IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> InitialiseTransferObject<TModel, TTransferObject>(this IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> mapper)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            mapper.MapListToMonitoringList();

            return mapper;
        }

        public static IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> MapListToMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>(this IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> mapper)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            Mapper.CreateMap<List<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<IEnumerable<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<IQueryable<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<ObservableCollection<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<IOrderedEnumerable<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);

            return mapper;
        }

        private static MonitoringList<TTransferObject> CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>(IEnumerable<TModel> source)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            try
            {
                if (source != null)
                {
                    return new MonitoringList<TTransferObject>(source.Select(Mapper.Map<TModel, TTransferObject>));
                }

                return null;

            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }

       public static void ResolveCodeDescription<TSource>(this IMemberConfigurationExpression<TSource> mce, 
                                                          Expression<Func<TSource, object>> code , Expression<Func<TSource, object>> description)
       {
            var codeProperty = ReflectionHelper.FindProperty(code).Name;
            var descriptionProperty = ReflectionHelper.FindProperty(description).Name;

            mce.ResolveUsing<CodeDescriptionResolver<TSource>>()
               .ConstructedBy(() => new CodeDescriptionResolver<TSource>(codeProperty, descriptionProperty)).FromMember(src => src);
       }

        public static void ResolveCodeDescription<TSource,TSubSource>(this IMemberConfigurationExpression<TSource> mce, Expression<Func<TSource, TSubSource>> source,
                                                           Expression<Func<TSubSource, object>> code, Expression<Func<TSubSource, object>> description)
        {
            var codeProperty =ReflectionHelper.FindProperty(code).Name;
            var descriptionProperty = ReflectionHelper.FindProperty(description).Name;
            var sourceMap = MakeFuncReturnObject(source);

            mce.ResolveUsing<CodeDescriptionResolver<TSubSource>>()
               .ConstructedBy(() => new CodeDescriptionResolver<TSubSource>(codeProperty, descriptionProperty)).FromMember(sourceMap);
        }

        public static void ResolveCodeDescription<TSource, TSubSource>(this IMemberConfigurationExpression<TSource> mce, Expression<Func<TSource, TSubSource>> source)
        {
            var sourceMap = MakeFuncReturnObject(source);

            mce.ResolveUsing<CodeDescriptionResolver<TSubSource>>()
               .ConstructedBy(() => new CodeDescriptionResolver<TSubSource>()).FromMember(sourceMap);
        }

        public static Expression<Func<TInType, object>> MakeFuncReturnObject<TInType, TOutType>(Expression<Func<TInType, TOutType>> expression)
       {
            var body = Expression.Convert(expression.Body, typeof(object));
            return Expression.Lambda<Func<TInType, object>>(body, expression.Parameters);
       }

        public static DictionaryResolver<T1, T2> Resolver<T1, T2>(this Dictionary<T1, T2> dictionary)
        {
            return new DictionaryResolver<T1, T2>(dictionary);
        }

        public static IResolutionExpression<T> ResolveUsing<T, T2, T3>(this IMemberConfigurationExpression<T> act, Dictionary<T2, T3> dictionary)
        {
            return act.ResolveUsing(dictionary.Resolver());
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 36/94: Orvex.Business/AutoMapper/AutoMapperExtensions.cs

using System.Linq;
using AutoMapper;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.DomainModel;
using Orvex.Model;
using OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig;
using OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig.Resolvers;

namespace Orvex.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig.Mapping
{
    public class KdpProfile : Profile
    {
        protected override void Configure()
        {
            this.ConfigureGetMappings();
        }

        private void ConfigureGetMappings()
        {
            Mapper.CreateMap<ChildExamination, KdpExamResultModel>()
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Id, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildExaminationId))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.OriginDesc, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.ExaminationOrigin()).FromMember(src => src.Origin.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminationCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkExaminationId))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ExamDescription, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminationDescriptions.LongDescription))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Contact, act => act.ResolveUsing(new CodeDescriptionResolver<ExaminationDescriptions>(nameof(ExaminationDescriptions.PkExamDescriptionId), nameof(ExaminationDescriptions.LongDescription))).FromMember(src => src.ExaminationDescriptions))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminationDate, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ExaminationDate))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.LocationType, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.FkLocationHeldTypeId))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminerTypeDesc, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.Examiner.Description))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ReasonNotExaminedDesc, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ExamNotExaminedReason.KdpDescription))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Consent, act => act.ResolveUsing<YesNoNullResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.Consent))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Reschedule, act => act.ResolveUsing<YesNoNullResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.RescheduleExamination))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.RskResponsiveParenting, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.RskResponsiveParenting))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.RskFamilyHealth, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.RskFamilyHealth))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.RskEngagement, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.RskEngagement))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.RskFamilySupport, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.RskFamilySupport))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.RskSocioFactors, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.RskSocioFactors))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.RskCompleted, act => act.ResolveUsing<YesNoNullResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.RskCompleted))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.OnwardReferralsMade, act => act.ResolveUsing<YesNoNullResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.OnwardReferralsMade))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ReceivingBoostSchemeService, act => act.ResolveUsing<YesNoNullResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.ReceivingBoostScheme))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.LookedAfterChild, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.YesNoUnknown(false, LookupsUtility.DICTIONARY_TYPE.STRING)).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.LookedAfterChild))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.OnChildProtectionRegister, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.YesNoUnknown(false, LookupsUtility.DICTIONARY_TYPE.STRING)).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.OnChildProtectionRegister))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.KdpCoreComponentOffered, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.CoreComponentOfferedDescription()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.CoreComponent.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.EvidenceOfXgm, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.EvidenceOfXgm()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.EvidenceOfXgm.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DomesticViolenceConcern, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.DomesticViolenceConcern()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DomesticViolenceConcern.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Development, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.DevelopmentAt15And27Months()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.Development.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DevelopmentFurther, act => act.ResolveUsing<YesNoNullResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DevelopmentFurther))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Heart, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.PhysicalAssessment6To8Weeks()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.Heart.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Hips, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.PhysicalAssessment6To8Weeks()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.Hips.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Eyes, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.PhysicalAssessment6To8Weeks()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.Eyes.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Testes, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.PhysicalAssessment6To8Weeks()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.Testes.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.PhysicalGeneral, act => act.ResolveUsing(LookupsUtility.PhysicalAssessment6To8Weeks()).FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.PhysicalGeneral.ToString()))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskLocomotor, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskLocomotor))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskCognitive, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskCognitive))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskHearingAndLanguage, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskHearingAndLanguage))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskInteractiveSocial, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskInteractiveSocial))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskManipulative, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskManipulative))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskSelfCareSocial, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskSelfCareSocial))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskSpeechAndLanguage, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskSpeechAndLanguage))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.DskVisual, act => act.ResolveUsing<KdpDskResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp.DskVisual))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.ChildMeasurementId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildMeasurement.SingleOrDefault(rec => rec.FkChildExaminationId == src.PkChildExaminationId).PkChildMeasurementId))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Height, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildMeasurement.SingleOrDefault(rec => rec.FkChildExaminationId == src.PkChildExaminationId).Height))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.Weight, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildMeasurement.SingleOrDefault(rec => rec.FkChildExaminationId == src.PkChildExaminationId).Weight))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.BodyMassIndex, act => act.ResolveUsing<BmiResolver>().FromMember(src => src.ChildMeasurement.SingleOrDefault(rec => rec.FkChildExaminationId == src.PkChildExaminationId)))
              .ForMember(dest => dest.HeadCircumference, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildMeasurement.SingleOrDefault(rec => rec.FkChildExaminationId == src.PkChildExaminationId).HeadCircumference))
              .IgnoreUnMapped();
        }
    }

namespace Orvex.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig.Resolvers
{
    public class BoolToStringResolver : ValueResolver<bool, string>

using System;
using AutoMapper;
using static Orvex.Resources.Resources;

namespace OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig.Resolvers
{
    public class KdpDskResolver : ValueResolver<string, string>
    {
        protected override string ResolveCore(string source)
        {
            return string.IsNullOrEmpty(source) ? string.Empty : (source == "N" ? NotAssessed : source);
        }
    }

namespace Orvex.Services.Internal

    public static class RepositoryExtensions
    {
        public static IQueryable<T> WhereIsActive<T>(this IQueryable<T> repository) where T : class, IActiveDateRange
        {
            return WhereIsActive(repository, DateTime.Now);
        }

        public static IQueryable<T> WhereIsActive<T>(this IQueryable<T> repository, DateTime checkDate) where T : class, IActiveDateRange
        {
            return repository.Where(ActiveDateRange.IsActive(checkDate))
                .Cast<T>();
        }
    }
