>>> FILE 42/94: Orvex.Business/Services/Business/ChildMeasurementsService.cs   [4. Business services]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.ServiceModel.Activation;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.DomainModel;
using Orvex.Model;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;

namespace Orvex.Business
{
    [AspNetCompatibilityRequirements(RequirementsMode = AspNetCompatibilityRequirementsMode.Allowed)]
    public class ChildMeasurementsService : IChildMeasurementsService
    {
        private readonly Func<IUnitOfWork> unitOfWorkFactory;

        public ChildMeasurementsService(Func<IUnitOfWork> unitOfWorkFactory)
        {
            this.unitOfWorkFactory = unitOfWorkFactory;
        }

        public decimal? CalculateBmi(decimal height, decimal weight)
        {
            var result = Math.Round(weight / ((height / 100) * (height / 100)), 2);
            decimal? roundedBmi = Convert.ToDecimal(result);
            if (roundedBmi > (decimal)99.99)
                roundedBmi = (decimal)99.99;

            return roundedBmi;
        }

        public List<ChildMeasurementsRange> GetPrescribedRanges()
        {
            var generalOptions = getGeneralOptions();
            return new List<ChildMeasurementsRange>
            {
                new ChildMeasurementsRange
                {
                    MeasurementType = "BMI",
                    UpperBound = (double)generalOptions.HBmiIndex,
                    LowerBound = (double)generalOptions.LBmiIndex,
                }
            };
        }

        public List<ChildMeasurementsRange> GetExpectedRanges(string gender, DateTime dateOfBirth, DateTime dateOfExamination)
        {
            var ageInDays = dateOfBirth.DaysBetween(dateOfExamination);

            var centileBands = getCentileBands(ageInDays, gender);

            return centileBands
                .GroupBy(cb => cb.MeasurementType)
                .Select(cb => new ChildMeasurementsRange
                {
                    MeasurementType = cb.Key,
                    UpperBound = cb.Max(b => b.Lower),
                    LowerBound = cb.Min(b => b.Upper)
                }).ToList();
        }

        private IEnumerable<DeviationsCentile> getCentileBands(int ageInDays, string gender)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                return uow.Repository<DeviationsCentile>().Where(d => d.Age == ageInDays && d.Gender == gender).ToList();
            }
        }

        private SystemGeneralOptions getGeneralOptions()
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                return uow.Repository<SystemGeneralOptions>().First();
            }
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 42/94: Orvex.Business/Services/Business/ChildMeasurementsService.cs

>>> FILE 43/94: Orvex.Business/Services/Business/Examinations/ExaminationsCrudManager.cs   [4. Business services]
====================================================================================================
using System.Data.Entity;
using System.Linq;
using Orvex.Model;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;

namespace Orvex.Business.Utils
{
    public class ExaminationsCrudManager
    {

        internal static void UpdateRepeatFlags(IUnitOfWork uow, long clientId, string examinationCode)
        {
            var examPrefix = examinationCode.Length > 2 ? examinationCode.Substring(0, 2) : examinationCode;

            //Query database to ensure all exam details are in local cache
            uow.Repository<ChildExaminationGiven>()
                .Include(e => e.ChildExamination)
                .Where(exam => exam.ChildExamination.ClientId == clientId && exam.ChildExamination.FkExaminationId.StartsWith(examPrefix))
                .ToList();

            //Set ChildExamination navigation property to allow querying against local cache
            foreach (var ceg in uow.Repository<ChildExaminationGiven>().Local().Where(ce => ce.ChildExamination == null))
            {
                ceg.ChildExamination = uow.Repository<ChildExamination>().Local().Single(ce => ce.PkChildExaminationId == ceg.PkFkChildExamGivenId);
            }
            
            //Fetch exams that where value of IsRepeat is incorrect and derive correct value
            var examsToUpdate = 
                from exam in uow.Repository<ChildExaminationGiven>().Local()
                where exam.ChildExamination.ClientId == clientId && exam.ChildExamination.FkExaminationId.StartsWith(examPrefix)
                group exam by exam.ChildExamination.FkExaminationId into grp
                let minExamDate = grp.Min(g => g.ExaminationDate)
                from g in grp
                where (g.ExaminationDate > minExamDate && g.ChildExamination.IsRepeat != "Y") || (g.ExaminationDate == minExamDate && g.ChildExamination.IsRepeat != "N")
                select new { g.ChildExamination, IsRepeat = (g.ExaminationDate == minExamDate) ? "N" : "Y" };

            foreach (var item in examsToUpdate)
            {
                item.ChildExamination.IsRepeat = item.IsRepeat;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Resets appointments if exam code has changed and clears any outstanding appointments with the same exam code
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="uow"></param>
        /// <param name="examinationCode"></param>
        /// <param name="clientId"></param>
        /// <param name="childExamId"></param>
        internal static void UpdateAppointmentStatuses(IUnitOfWork uow, string examinationCode, long clientId, long childExamId)
        {
            ResetAppointmentsIfExamCodeChanged(uow, examinationCode, clientId, childExamId);
            ClearOutstandingAppointments(uow, examinationCode, clientId, childExamId);
        }

        internal static void ClearOutstandingAppointments(IUnitOfWork uow, string examinationCode, long clientId, long childExamId)
        {
            // Set any outstanding appointments for this examination code to not outstanding
            uow.Repository<HlxAppointment>()
                .Where(ca => ca.ClientId == clientId
                    && ca.ExaminationDue == examinationCode
                    && ca.IsOutstanding != "N")
                .ToList()
                .ForEach(item => {
                    item.IsOutstanding = "N";
                    item.FkChildExaminationId = childExamId;
                });
        }

        internal static void ResetAppointmentsIfExamCodeChanged(IUnitOfWork uow, string examinationCode, long clientId, long childExamId)
        {
            // Check if any exams have previously been set to not outstanding using this result but a different exam code
            // (this could happen if the exam code of the result has been updated) - if so set these to outstanding again
            uow.Repository<HlxAppointment>()
                .Where(ca => ca.ClientId == clientId
                    && ca.FkChildExaminationId == childExamId
                    && ca.ExaminationDue != examinationCode
                    && ca.IsOutstanding != "Y")
                .ToList()
                .ForEach(item => {
                    item.IsOutstanding = "Y";
                    item.FkChildExaminationId = null;
                });
        }

        internal static void ResetAppointmentsForDeletedResult(IUnitOfWork uow, string examinationCode, long clientId, long childExamId)
        {
            // Set any appointments previously set to not outstanding using this result back to outstanding
            uow.Repository<HlxAppointment>()
                .Where(ca => ca.ClientId == clientId
                    && ca.ExaminationDue == examinationCode
                    && ca.IsOutstanding != "Y"
                    && ca.FkChildExaminationId == childExamId)
                .ToList()
                .ForEach(item => {
                    item.IsOutstanding = "Y";
                    item.FkChildExaminationId = null;
                });
        }

        internal static void ClearProducedRecalls(IUnitOfWork uow, string examinationCode, long clientId)
        {
            const string ProducedFlagPreschool = "A";
            const string ProducedFlagSchool = "P";

            uow.Repository<ChildPreschoolRecall>()
                .Where(r => r.FkClientId == clientId && r.FkExamCode == examinationCode && r.RecallIndicator == ProducedFlagPreschool)
                .ToList()
                .ForEach(r => { r.RecordStatus = "D"; });

            uow.Repository<ChildSchoolRecall>()
                .Where(r => r.ClientId == clientId && r.ExamCode == examinationCode && r.RecallIndicator == ProducedFlagSchool)
                .ToList()
                .ForEach(r => { r.RecordStatus = "D"; });
        }
    }
}

namespace Orvex.Business

    [AspNetCompatibilityRequirements(RequirementsMode = AspNetCompatibilityRequirementsMode.Allowed)]
    public class KdpExaminationService: IKdpExaminationService
    {
        private readonly Func<IUnitOfWork> unitOfWorkFactory;
        private readonly GetCurrentDateTime getCurrentDateTime;

        private const byte PreschoolConsentCategory = (byte)LookupsUtility.CONSENT_CATEGORY.PSH;
        private readonly string kdpConsentYesValue = LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT];
        private static readonly string KdpExamCodeAcronym = LookupsUtility.ExamTypeCodeMap()[ExamType.Kdp];
        private const byte MedicalConsentType = 11;


        private readonly KdpExaminationValidator kdpExaminationValidator;
        static KdpExaminationService()
        {
            ConfigureMappings();
        }

        public static void ConfigureMappings()
        {
            #region CreateMap Model -->  Transfer Object
            Mapper.CreateMap<ChildExamination, KdpExaminationResultTO>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkChildExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ClientId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ClientId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminationCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.OriginalExamCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Sector, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Sector))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ExamDescription, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminationDescriptions.LongDescription))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.IsRepeat, act => act.ResolveUsing<StringToBoolResolver>().FromMember(src => src.IsRepeat))
                .IgnoreUnMapped();

            Mapper.CreateMap<ChildExaminationGiven, KdpExaminationResultTO>()
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.PkChildExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkFkChildExamGivenId))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.LocationType, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkLocationHeldTypeId))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.LocationCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkChildLocationHeldCode))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminerType, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkStaffTypeExaminerId))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminerHcpCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkHcpExaminerCode))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminerCategory, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkExaminerCatId))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.LocationName, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildLocations.Name))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.ReasonNotExamined, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkNotExaminedReasonId))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminerName, act => act.MapFrom(src => string.Join(" ", src.HealthCareProfessionals.Name1, src.HealthCareProfessionals.Name2)))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.Reschedule, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.RescheduleExamination))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminerTypeDescription, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Examiner.Description))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.HeightMeasurementDeclined, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.HeightMeasurementRefused))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.WeightMeasurementDeclined, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.WeightMeasurementRefused))
                 .ForMember(dest => dest.HeadMeasurementDeclined, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.HeadMeasurementRefused))
                 .IgnoreUnMapped();

            Mapper.CreateMap<ChildExaminationKdp, KdpExaminationResultTO>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkChildExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkFkExamKdpGivenId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ReceivingBoostSchemeService, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ReceivingBoostScheme))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ReasonNotExaminedDescription, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildExaminationGiven.ExamNotExaminedReason.KdpDescription))
                .IgnoreUnMapped();

            Mapper.CreateMap<ChildMeasurement, KdpExaminationResultTO>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ChildMeasurementId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildMeasurementId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkChildExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.FkChildExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ClientId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ClientId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Sector, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Sector))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminationDate, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.MeasurementDate))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Height, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Height))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Weight, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Weight))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.HeadCircumference, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.HeadCircumference))
                .IgnoreUnMapped();

            Mapper.CreateMap<PreSchoolExams_NextExamDue_ProcResults, NextExamDueTO>();

            #endregion

            #region CreateMap Transfer Object --> Model
            Mapper.CreateMap<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExamination>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkChildExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ClientId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ClientId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminationCode))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Sector, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Sector))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.IsRepeat, act => act.ResolveUsing<BoolToStringResolver>().FromMember(src => src.IsRepeat))
                .MapIntoUnitOfWork(model => model.PkChildExaminationId, to => to.PkChildExaminationId);

            Mapper.CreateMap<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationGiven>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkFkChildExamGivenId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ExaminationDate, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminationDate))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkLocationHeldTypeId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.LocationType))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkChildLocationHeldCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.LocationCode))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkStaffTypeExaminerId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminerType))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkHcpExaminerCode, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminerHcpCode))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkExaminerCatId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminerCategory))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkNotExaminedReasonId, act => act.MapFrom(src => string.IsNullOrEmpty(src.ReasonNotExamined)
                    ? (byte?)null
                    : Convert.ToByte(src.ReasonNotExamined)))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.RescheduleExamination, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Reschedule))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.HeightMeasurementRefused, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.HeightMeasurementDeclined))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.WeightMeasurementRefused, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.WeightMeasurementDeclined))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.HeadMeasurementRefused, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.HeadMeasurementDeclined))
                .MapIntoUnitOfWork(model => model.PkFkChildExamGivenId, to => to.PkChildExaminationId);

            Mapper.CreateMap<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationKdp>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkFkExamKdpGivenId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ReceivingBoostScheme, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ReceivingBoostSchemeService))
                .MapIntoUnitOfWork(model => model.PkFkExamKdpGivenId, to => to.PkChildExaminationId);

            Mapper.CreateMap<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildMeasurement>()
                .ForMember(dest => dest.PkChildMeasurementId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ChildMeasurementId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.FkChildExaminationId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.PkChildExaminationId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.ClientId, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ClientId))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Sector, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Sector))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.MeasurementDate, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.ExaminationDate))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Height, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Height))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.Weight, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.Weight))
                .ForMember(dest => dest.HeadCircumference, act => act.MapFrom(src => src.HeadCircumference))
                .MapIntoUnitOfWork(model => model.PkChildMeasurementId, to => to.ChildMeasurementId);
            #endregion
        }

        public KdpExaminationService(Func<IUnitOfWork> unitOfWorkFactory,
            KdpExaminationValidator kdpExamValidator, GetCurrentDateTime getCurrentDateTime)
        {
            this.unitOfWorkFactory = unitOfWorkFactory;
            this.getCurrentDateTime = getCurrentDateTime;
            kdpExaminationValidator = kdpExamValidator;
        }

        public KdpExaminationTO GetKdpExamination(long clientId)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var childExamination = uow.Repository<ChildExamination>()
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildMeasurement)
                    .Include(c => c.ExaminationDescriptions)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildLocations)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.HealthCareProfessionals)
                    .Where(c => c.ClientId == clientId && c.ExaminationDescriptions.FkExamDescriptionType == ExamType.Kdp)
                    .OrderByDescending(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ExaminationDate)
                    .ToList();

                var childDetails = GetChildDetails(clientId, uow);

                var outstandingResults = GetKdpOutstandingAppointments(clientId, uow);

                return new KdpExaminationTO
                {
                    Id = clientId.ToString("D"),
                    DateOfBirth = childDetails.DateOfBirth,
                    DateOfDeath = childDetails.DateOfDeath,
                    Gender = childDetails.Sex,
                    HasPreschoolConsent = childDetails.Consents.SingleOrDefault(con => con.ConsentCategory == PreschoolConsentCategory && con.ConsentType == MedicalConsentType)?.ConsentResult == kdpConsentYesValue,
                    HcpExaminationResultToList = childExamination.Select(GetKdpResultTo).ToList(),
                    OutstandingAppointmentsList = outstandingResults
                };
            }
        }

        private static List<ChildExaminationGivenSubTO> GetKdpOutstandingAppointments(long clientId, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var outstandingAppointments = uow.Repository<HlxAppointment>()
               .Include(appt => appt.ExaminationDescriptions)
               .Where(appt => appt.ClientId == clientId
                              && appt.ExaminationDue.StartsWith(KdpExamCodeAcronym)
                              && appt.IsOutstanding == "Y"
                              && appt.AppointmentDatetime <= DateTime.Now)
               .OrderByDescending(appt => appt.AppointmentDatetime)
               .ToList();

            return outstandingAppointments.Select(appt => new ChildExaminationGivenSubTO
            {
                PkChildExaminationGivenId = 0,
                ClientId = clientId,
                ExaminationCode = appt.ExaminationDue,
                ExaminationDesc = appt.ExaminationDescriptions?.ShortDescription,
                ExaminationDate = appt.AppointmentDatetime,
                Origin = LookupsUtility.EXAMINATION_ORIGIN.SCHEDULED
            }).ToList();
        }

        private static ChildGeneral GetChildDetails(long clientId, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            return uow.Repository<ChildGeneral>()
                .Include(cg => cg.Consents)
                .Single(cg => cg.ClientId == clientId);
        }

        public static KdpExaminationResultTO GetKdpResultTo(ChildExamination childExamination)
        {
            var kdpResultTo = childExamination.To<KdpExaminationResultTO>();

            if (childExamination.ChildExaminationGiven != null)
            {
                Mapper.Map(childExamination.ChildExaminationGiven, kdpResultTo);

                if (childExamination.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp != null)
                {
                    Mapper.Map(childExamination.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp, kdpResultTo);
                }
            }

            var childMeasurement = childExamination.ChildMeasurement.SingleOrDefault();
            if (childMeasurement != null)
            {
                Mapper.Map(childMeasurement, kdpResultTo);
            }
            kdpResultTo.GenerateLock();
            kdpResultTo.PropertyChanged.Clear();
            return kdpResultTo;
        }

        public KdpExaminationResultTO GetKdpExaminationResult(long pkChildExaminationId)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var childExamination = uow.Repository<ChildExamination>()
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildMeasurement)
                    .Include(c => c.ExaminationDescriptions)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildLocations)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.HealthCareProfessionals)
                    .FirstOrDefault(c => c.PkChildExaminationId == pkChildExaminationId);
          
                return GetKdpResultTo(childExamination);
            }
        }

        public void SaveKdpExaminationResult(KdpExaminationTO kdpExamTo, KdpExaminationResultTO kdpExamResultTo)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var currentData = uow.Repository<ChildExamination>()
                    .Include(ce => ce.ChildExaminationGiven)
                    .Include(ce => ce.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp)
                    .Include(ce => ce.ChildMeasurement)
                    .SingleOrDefault(c => c.PkChildExaminationId == kdpExamResultTo.PkChildExaminationId);

                if (currentData != null)
                {
                    kdpExamResultTo.CheckLock(GetKdpResultTo(currentData));
                    kdpExaminationValidator.ValidateOnSave(kdpExamResultTo, kdpExamTo.DateOfBirth, kdpExamTo.DateOfDeath,kdpExamTo.HcpExaminationResultToList);
                    UpdateKdpExaminationResult(uow, kdpExamResultTo, currentData);
                }
                else
                {
                    kdpExaminationValidator.ValidateOnSave(kdpExamResultTo, kdpExamTo.DateOfBirth, kdpExamTo.DateOfDeath,kdpExamTo.HcpExaminationResultToList);
                    InsertKdpExaminationResult(uow, kdpExamResultTo);
                }

                ExaminationsCrudManager.UpdateRepeatFlags(uow, kdpExamResultTo.ClientId,kdpExamResultTo.ExaminationCode);

                ExaminationsCrudManager.UpdateAppointmentStatuses(uow, kdpExamResultTo.ExaminationCode, kdpExamResultTo.ClientId, kdpExamResultTo.PkChildExaminationId);

                ExaminationsCrudManager.ClearProducedRecalls(uow, kdpExamResultTo.ExaminationCode, kdpExamResultTo.ClientId);

                uow.Commit();
            }
        }

        private void UpdateKdpExaminationResult(IUnitOfWork uow, KdpExaminationResultTO kdpExamResultTo, ChildExamination currentData)
        {
            uow.UpdateTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExamination>(kdpExamResultTo, checkLock: false);

            kdpExamResultTo.ResultEnteredDate = DateTime.Now;
            if (currentData.ChildExaminationGiven != null)
                uow.UpdateTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationGiven>(kdpExamResultTo,checkLock: false);
            else uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationGiven>(kdpExamResultTo);

            if(currentData.ChildExaminationGiven?.ChildExaminationKdp !=null)
                uow.UpdateTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationKdp>(kdpExamResultTo, checkLock: false);
            else uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationKdp>(kdpExamResultTo);

            if (kdpExamResultTo.ChildMeasurementId > 0)
            {
                uow.UpdateTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildMeasurement>(kdpExamResultTo, checkLock: false);
            }else if ((kdpExamResultTo.Height.HasValue || kdpExamResultTo.Weight.HasValue || kdpExamResultTo.HeadCircumference.HasValue) &&
                      kdpExamResultTo.ChildMeasurementId <= 0)
            {
                kdpExamResultTo.ChildMeasurementId = GetNextChildMeasurementPrimaryKey(uow);
                uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildMeasurement>(kdpExamResultTo);
            }
        }

        private void InsertKdpExaminationResult(IUnitOfWork uow, KdpExaminationResultTO kdpExamResultTo)
        {
            kdpExamResultTo.PkChildExaminationId = GetChildExaminationPrimaryKey(uow);

            uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExamination>(kdpExamResultTo);
            uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationGiven>(kdpExamResultTo);
            uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildExaminationKdp>(kdpExamResultTo);


            if ((kdpExamResultTo.Height.HasValue || kdpExamResultTo.Weight.HasValue || kdpExamResultTo.HeadCircumference.HasValue) && kdpExamResultTo.ChildMeasurementId <= 0)
            {
                kdpExamResultTo.ChildMeasurementId = GetNextChildMeasurementPrimaryKey(uow);
                uow.InsertTransferObject<KdpExaminationResultTO, ChildMeasurement>(kdpExamResultTo);
            }
        }

        private static long GetChildExaminationPrimaryKey(IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            return uow.GetNextId<ChildExamination>();
        }

        private static long GetNextChildMeasurementPrimaryKey(IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            return uow.GetNextId<ChildMeasurement>();
        }

        public void DeleteKdpExaminationResult(long examinationId, long clientId, string examinationCode)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var result = uow.Repository<ChildExamination>()
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildMeasurement)
                    .SingleOrDefault(c => c.PkChildExaminationId == examinationId);

                if (result == null)
                    throw OptimisticLockFault.Default();

                result.ChildMeasurement.ToList().ForEach(uow.Repository<ChildMeasurement>().Delete);
                uow.Repository<ChildExaminationKdp>().Delete(result.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp);
                uow.Repository<ChildExaminationGiven>().Delete(result.ChildExaminationGiven);
                uow.Repository<ChildExamination>().Delete(result);

                ExaminationsCrudManager.UpdateRepeatFlags(uow, clientId, examinationCode);
                ExaminationsCrudManager.ResetAppointmentsForDeletedResult(uow, examinationCode, Convert.ToInt64(clientId), examinationId);

                uow.Commit();
            }
        }

        public void SaveScheduledResult(KdpScheduledResultTO kdpExamResultTo)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var resultAlreadyRecorded = uow.Repository<HlxAppointment>()
                    .Single(appt => appt.PkHlxAppointmentId == kdpExamResultTo.AppointmentId)
                    .IsOutstanding == HLXBoolean.False;

                if (resultAlreadyRecorded)
                {
                    throw OptimisticLockFault.Default();
                }

                kdpExaminationValidator.ValidateOnSave(kdpExamResultTo, kdpExamResultTo.DateOfBirth, kdpExamResultTo.DateOfDeath);

                InsertKdpExaminationResult(uow, kdpExamResultTo);

                ExaminationsCrudManager.UpdateAppointmentStatuses(uow, kdpExamResultTo.ExaminationCode, kdpExamResultTo.ClientId, kdpExamResultTo.PkChildExaminationId);

                ExaminationsCrudManager.ClearProducedRecalls(uow, kdpExamResultTo.ExaminationCode, kdpExamResultTo.ClientId);

                if (kdpExamResultTo.WithdrawConsent)
                {
                    var medicalConsent = uow.Repository<Consent>()
                        .Single(c => c.ClientId == kdpExamResultTo.ClientId
                            && c.ConsentCategory == (byte)LookupsUtility.CONSENT_CATEGORY.PSH
                            && c.ConsentType == MedicalConsentType);

                    medicalConsent.ConsentResult = LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT_REFUSED];
                    medicalConsent.DateOfConsent = getCurrentDateTime().Date;
                }

                uow.Commit();
            }
        }

        public List<KdpExamResultModel> GetKdpExamResults(long clientId)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var childGeneral = uow.Repository<ChildGeneral>().Single(rec => rec.ClientId == clientId);

                var childExaminationList = uow.Repository<ChildExamination>()
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ChildExaminationKdp)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.Examiner)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ExamNotExaminedReason)
                    .Include(c => c.ChildMeasurement)
                    .Include(c => c.ExaminationDescriptions)
                    .Where(c => c.ClientId == childGeneral.ClientId &&
                            c.ExaminationDescriptions.FkExamDescriptionType == ExamType.Kdp)
                    .OrderByDescending(c => c.ChildExaminationGiven.ExaminationDate)
                    .ToList();
                var kdpExamResultModelList = Mapper.Map<List<ChildExamination>, List<KdpExamResultModel>>(childExaminationList);

                var resultsLocationTypes = kdpExamResultModelList.Where(result => result.LocationType != null).Select(result => result.LocationType).Distinct();

                var locationTypes = resultsLocationTypes as IList<byte?> ?? resultsLocationTypes.ToList();
                if (!locationTypes.Any()) return kdpExamResultModelList;
                var kdpLocationTypes = uow.Repository<KdpLocationTypes>().Where(rec => locationTypes.Contains(rec.FkLocationType)).ToList();
                foreach (var kdpExamResultModel in kdpExamResultModelList.Where(kdpExamResultModel => kdpExamResultModel.LocationType != null))
                {
                    kdpExamResultModel.LocationTypeDesc = kdpLocationTypes.SingleOrDefault(rec => rec.FkLocationType == kdpExamResultModel.LocationType)?.Description;
                }
                return kdpExamResultModelList;
            }
        }

        public Tuple<double, double> GetAgeRangeFromExamSchedule(string examCode)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                var scheduledExam = uow.Repository<PreschoolScheduleExams>()
                                        .WhereIsActive()
                                        .Where(x => x.FkExamCode == examCode)
                                        .Select(x => new { MinimumAgeWks = x.MinimumAgeWks, MaximumAgeWks = x.MaximumAgeWks })
                                        .FirstOrDefault();

                if (scheduledExam == null)
                    return null;

                var minimumAgeInWeeks = Convert.ToDouble(scheduledExam.MinimumAgeWks);
                var maximumAgeInWeeks = (double?)scheduledExam.MaximumAgeWks ?? double.MaxValue;

                return new Tuple<double,double>(minimumAgeInWeeks, maximumAgeInWeeks);
            }
        }

        public NextExamDueTO GetNextKdpContactDue(long clientId)
        {
            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                return Mapper.Map<NextExamDueTO>(PreschoolsManager.NextPreschoolExamDue(clientId, uow).FirstOrDefault());
            }
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 44/94: Orvex.Business/Services/Business/Examinations/KdpExaminationService.cs

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Linq;
using HLX.Net2008.Common;
using Orvex.Contracts.Enums;
using Orvex.Model.ProcResults;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using Oracle.ManagedDataAccess.Client;

namespace Orvex.Business.Utils
{
    public class PreschoolsManager
    {
        public static event HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolRunProgress PreschoolRunProgressChanged;

        private static decimal appointmentRunId = 0;

        public static List<PreSchoolExams_NextExamDue_ProcResults> NextPreschoolExamDue(decimal clientID, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var pClientID = new OracleParameter("pclientID", clientID);
            var pSector = new OracleParameter("pSector", GlobalSessionValues.Sector);
            var pDataset = new OracleParameter("pDataset", OracleDbType.RefCursor);
            pDataset.Direction = ParameterDirection.Output;

            return uow.SqlQuery<PreSchoolExams_NextExamDue_ProcResults>("begin PRESCHOOLEXAMS.NEXT_EXAM_DUE(:pClientID, :pSector, :pDataset); end;", pClientID, pSector, pDataset).ToList();
        }

        public static List<PreSchoolExams_ClinicList_ProcResults> GetClinicList(string pageType, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var pPageType = new OracleParameter("pPageType", pageType);
            var pDataset = new OracleParameter("pDataset", OracleDbType.RefCursor);
            pDataset.Direction = ParameterDirection.Output;

            return uow.SqlQuery<PreSchoolExams_ClinicList_ProcResults>("begin PRESCHOOLEXAMS.CLINIC_LIST(:pPageType, :pDataset); end;", pPageType, pDataset).ToList();
        }

        public static List<Immunisations_GetAppointmentsForRun_ProcResults> GetAppointmentsForRun(decimal appointmentRunID, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var pAppointmentRunID = new OracleParameter("pAppointmentRunID", appointmentRunID);
            var pDataset = new OracleParameter("pDataset", OracleDbType.RefCursor);
            pDataset.Direction = ParameterDirection.Output;

            return uow.SqlQuery<Immunisations_GetAppointmentsForRun_ProcResults>("begin PRESCHOOLEXAMS.GET_APPOINTMENTS_FOR_RUN(:pAppointmentRunID, :pDataset); end;", pAppointmentRunID, pDataset).ToList();
        }

        public static List<Preschools_GetAppointmentQueueForRun_ProcResults> GetAppointmentsQueueForRun(decimal appointmentRunID, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var pAppointmentRunId = new OracleParameter("pAppointmentRunID", appointmentRunID);
            var pDataset = new OracleParameter("pDataset", OracleDbType.RefCursor)
            {
                Direction = ParameterDirection.Output
            };

            return uow.SqlQuery<Preschools_GetAppointmentQueueForRun_ProcResults>("begin PRESCHOOLEXAMS.GET_APPOINTMENT_QUEUE_FOR_RUN(:pAppointmentRunID, :pDataset); end;", pAppointmentRunId, pDataset).ToList();
        }

        public static int GenerateAppointments(IUnitOfWork uow, long appointmentRunId)
        {
            OracleDependencies(uow.GetConnection() as OracleConnection, appointmentRunId);

            var pUserId = new OracleParameter("P_USER_ID", GlobalSessionValues.UUID);
            var pAppointmentRunId = new OracleParameter("P_APPOINTMENT_RUN_ID", appointmentRunId);
            var pResult = new OracleParameter("P_RESULT", OracleDbType.Int64, ParameterDirection.ReturnValue);
            uow.ExecuteSqlCommand("begin :res := PRESCHOOLEXAMS.APPOINTMENT_RUN(:P_USER_ID, :P_APPOINTMENT_RUN_ID); end;", pResult, pUserId, pAppointmentRunId);

            if (pResult.Value != null)
            {
                return Convert.ToInt32(pResult.Value.ToString());
            }

            return 0;
        }

        private static void OracleDependencies(OracleConnection oracleConnection, decimal appointmentRunId)
        {
            ReadProgressUpdate((int)HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.ProgressStarted, appointmentRunId, oraDep_Start, oracleConnection);
            ReadProgressUpdate((int)HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.SessionsIdentified, appointmentRunId, oraDep_SessionIdentified, oracleConnection);
            ReadProgressUpdate((int)HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.ChildrenIdentified, appointmentRunId, oraDep_ChildrenIdentified, oracleConnection);
            ReadProgressUpdate((int)HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.ChildrenAppointed, appointmentRunId, oraDep_Appointed, oracleConnection);
            ReadProgressUpdate((int)HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.RunComplete, appointmentRunId, oraDep_Complete, oracleConnection);
        }

        public static OracleDependency ReadProgressUpdate(int progressType, decimal appointmentRunId, OnChangeEventHandler eventHandler, OracleConnection oracleConnection)
        {
            var oraCmd = oracleConnection.CreateCommand();

            oraCmd.CommandText = "select appointment_run_progress_type" +
                                 " from appdata1.hlx_appointment_run_progress" +
                                 " where appointment_run_progress_type =" + progressType +
                                 " and Fk_Hlx_Appointment_Run_Id = " + appointmentRunId;

            var oraDependency = new OracleDependency(oraCmd);
            oraCmd.AddRowid = true;
            oraDependency.OnChange += eventHandler;
            oraCmd.ExecuteNonQuery();

            return oraDependency;
        }

        public static void oraDep_Start(object sender, OracleNotificationEventArgs eventArgs)
        {
            PreschoolRunProgressChanged(sender, new HLXCustomEvents.AppointmentRunProgressChangedEventArgs(HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.ProgressStarted, "Progress Started"));
        }

        public static void oraDep_SessionIdentified(object sender, OracleNotificationEventArgs eventArgs)
        {
            PreschoolRunProgressChanged(sender, new HLXCustomEvents.AppointmentRunProgressChangedEventArgs(HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.SessionsIdentified, "Sessions Identified"));
        }

        public static void oraDep_ChildrenIdentified(object sender, OracleNotificationEventArgs eventArgs)
        {
            PreschoolRunProgressChanged(sender, new HLXCustomEvents.AppointmentRunProgressChangedEventArgs(HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.ChildrenIdentified, "Children Identified"));
        }

        public static void oraDep_Appointed(object sender, OracleNotificationEventArgs eventArgs)
        {
            PreschoolRunProgressChanged(sender, new HLXCustomEvents.AppointmentRunProgressChangedEventArgs(HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.ChildrenAppointed, "Children Appointed"));
        }

        public static void oraDep_Complete(object sender, OracleNotificationEventArgs eventArgs)
        {
            PreschoolRunProgressChanged(sender, new HLXCustomEvents.AppointmentRunProgressChangedEventArgs(HLXCustomEvents.PreschoolProgressType.RunComplete, "Run Complete"));
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 46/94: Orvex.Business/Services/Business/Lookups/LookupService.cs   [4. Business services]

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Linq;
using System.ServiceModel.Activation;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.Contracts.Enums;
using Orvex.Model;
using Orvex.Model.ProcResults;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using Oracle.ManagedDataAccess.Client;

namespace Orvex.Business
{
    [AspNetCompatibilityRequirements(RequirementsMode = AspNetCompatibilityRequirementsMode.Allowed)]
    public class LookupService : ILookupService
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<TypeOfLookup, List<LookupsSubTO>> cachedLookups = new Dictionary<TypeOfLookup, List<LookupsSubTO>>();
        private static readonly Dictionary<TypeOfLookup, List<LookupsSubTO>> cachedLookupsByUsed = new Dictionary<TypeOfLookup, List<LookupsSubTO>>();

        private readonly Func<IUnitOfWork> unitOfWorkFactory;

        public LookupService(Func<IUnitOfWork> unitOfWorkFactory)
        {
            this.unitOfWorkFactory = unitOfWorkFactory;
        }

        public void RefreshCache()
        {
            cachedLookups.Clear();
            cachedLookupsByUsed.Clear();
        }

        public List<LookupsSubTO> getLookups(TypeOfLookup lookUpType, string id, string type)
        {
            var isCachingType = string.IsNullOrEmpty(id) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(type);

            if (isCachingType && cachedLookups.ContainsKey(lookUpType))
            {
                return cachedLookups[lookUpType];
            }

            var lookupValues = Query(lookUpType, id, type, null) ?? new List<LookupsSubTO>();

            if (isCachingType)
            {
                cachedLookups.Add(lookUpType, lookupValues);
            }

            return lookupValues;
        }

        public List<LookupsSubTO> getLookupsByUsed(TypeOfLookup lookUpType, string id, string type, string used, bool allowCached = true)
        {
            var isCachingType = allowCached && string.IsNullOrEmpty(id) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(type) && used == "Y";

            if (isCachingType && cachedLookupsByUsed.ContainsKey(lookUpType))
            {
                return cachedLookupsByUsed[lookUpType];
            }

            var lookupValues = Query(lookUpType, id, type, used) ?? new List<LookupsSubTO>();

            if (isCachingType)
            {
                cachedLookupsByUsed.Add(lookUpType, lookupValues);
            }

            return lookupValues;
        }

        private List<LookupsSubTO> Query(TypeOfLookup lookupType, string specificCode, string specificType, string used)
        {
            var includeUnUsed = used != null && used != "Y";

            using (var uow = unitOfWorkFactory())
            {
                switch (lookupType)
                {
                    case TypeOfLookup.Examiners:
                        return GetExaminers(!includeUnUsed, specificType, specificCode, uow);
                    case TypeOfLookup.ExaminerByPrimaryKeyKdp:
                        return GetExaminerLookupWithPrimaryKeyAsCode(uow, LookupsUtility.ExaminerType.KDP);
                    case TypeOfLookup.Locations:
                        return GetLocations(true, specificType, specificCode, uow);
                    case TypeOfLookup.ExamLocationTypeKdp:
                        return LookupKdpLocationTypes(uow);
                    case TypeOfLookup.ExamNotExaminedReasonKdp:
                        return LookupExamNotExaminedReasonKdp(uow);
                    case TypeOfLookup.AllExams:
                        return LookupExamDescriptions(uow, specificCode, specificType);
                    default:
                        return null;
                }
            }
        }

        private static List<LookupsSubTO> GetExaminers(bool usedCodesOnly, string examinerType, string lookBySpecificCode, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var used = usedCodesOnly && string.IsNullOrEmpty(lookBySpecificCode) ? "Y" : "N";

            var pUsed = new OracleParameter("pUsed", used);
            var pExaminerType = new OracleParameter("pExaminerType", examinerType);
            var pCode = new OracleParameter("pCode", lookBySpecificCode);
            var pDataset = new OracleParameter("pDataset", OracleDbType.RefCursor) { Direction = ParameterDirection.Output };

            var result = uow.SqlQuery<Lookups_GetExaminer_ProcResults>("begin LOOKUPS.GET_EXAMINER(:pUsed, :pExaminerType, :pCode, :pDataset); end;", pUsed, pExaminerType, pCode, pDataset);
            return result.Where(r => r.IS_ACTIVE == 1)
                .Select(r => new LookupsSubTO { Code = r.CODE, Description = r.DESCRIPTION, Type = r.TYPE, UsedCode = r.USED_CODE })
                .ToList();
        }

        private static List<LookupsSubTO> GetExaminerLookupWithPrimaryKeyAsCode(IUnitOfWork uow, LookupsUtility.ExaminerType examinerType)
        {
            return uow.Repository<Examiner>().Where(e => e.ExaminerType == examinerType.ToString() || examinerType == LookupsUtility.ExaminerType.ALL)
                .OrderBy(examiner => examiner.PkExaminerId)
                .Select(r => new LookupsSubTO { Code = r.PkExaminerId, Description = r.Description })
                .ToList();
        }

        private static List<LookupsSubTO> GetLocations(bool usedCodesOnly, string locationType, string lookBySpecificCode, IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            var pUsed = new OracleParameter("pUsed", usedCodesOnly ? "Y" : "N");
            var pLocType = new OracleParameter("pLocationType", locationType);
            var pCode = new OracleParameter("pCode", lookBySpecificCode);
            var pDataset = new OracleParameter("pDataset", OracleDbType.RefCursor) { Direction = ParameterDirection.Output };

            var result = uow.SqlQuery<Lookups_GetLocations_ProcResults>("begin LOOKUPS.GET_LOCATIONS(:pUsed, :pLocationType, :pCode, :pDataset); end;", pUsed, pLocType, pCode, pDataset);

            return result.Select(dr => new LookupsSubTO
            {
                Code = dr.CODE,
                Description = dr.DESCRIPTION,
                Type = dr.TYPE.ToString(),
                LinkedOrganisation = dr.LINKED_ORGANISATION,
                SubType = dr.SUB_TYPE.ToString(),
                Address1 = dr.ADD1,
                Address2 = dr.ADD2,
                Address3 = dr.ADD3,
                Address4 = dr.ADD4,
                Address5 = dr.ADD5,
                Postcode = dr.POSTCODE,
                Telephone = dr.TELEPHONE,
                AddressLines = GetAddressLines(dr.ADD1, dr.ADD2, dr.ADD3, dr.ADD4, dr.ADD5, dr.POSTCODE)
            }).ToList();
        }

        private static string GetAddressLines(params string[] addressParts)
        {
            return string.Join("," + Environment.NewLine, addressParts.Where(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)));
        }

        private static List<LookupsSubTO> LookupKdpLocationTypes(IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            return uow.Repository<KdpLocationTypes>()
                .OrderBy(hlt => hlt.Code)
                .Select(hlt => new LookupsSubTO
                {
                    Code = hlt.FkLocationType.ToString(),
                    Description = hlt.Description
                }).ToList();
        }

        private static List<LookupsSubTO> LookupExamNotExaminedReasonKdp(IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            return uow.Repository<ExamNotExaminedReason>()
                .Where(ener => ener.KdpDescription != null)
                .Select(r => new LookupsSubTO { Code = r.PkExamNotExamRsonId.ToString(), Description = r.KdpDescription })
                .ToList();
        }

        private static List<LookupsSubTO> LookupExamDescriptions(IUnitOfWork uow, string examCode, string examType)
        {
            var filteredExams = uow.Repository<ExaminationDescriptions>().AsQueryable();

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(examType))
            {
                var examTypeEnum = (ExamType)Enum.Parse(typeof(ExamType), examType);
                filteredExams = filteredExams.Where(ed => ed.FkExamDescriptionType == examTypeEnum);
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(examCode))
                filteredExams = filteredExams.Where(ed => ed.PkExamDescriptionId == examCode);

            return filteredExams
                .Select(ed => new LookupsSubTO
                {
                    Code = ed.PkExamDescriptionId,
                    Description = ed.LongDescription,
                    ShortDescription = ed.ShortDescription,
                    ActiveFrom = ed.ActiveFrom,
                    ActiveTo = ed.ActiveTo
                }).ToList();
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 46/94: Orvex.Business/Services/Business/Lookups/LookupService.cs

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Orvex.Model;

namespace Orvex.Services.Internal
{
    public static class UnitOfWorkSequenceExtensions
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<Type, string> PrimaryKeySequenceMappings = new Dictionary<Type, string>
        {
            { typeof(ChildExamination), "CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID_SEQ" },
            { typeof(ChildMeasurement), "CHILD_MEASUREMENT_ID_SEQ" }
        };

        public static long GetNextId<TModel>(this IUnitOfWork uow)
        {
            string sequenceName;

            if (!PrimaryKeySequenceMappings.TryGetValue(typeof(TModel), out sequenceName))
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException("There is no primary key sequence name mapped to the given model type");
            }

            return uow.SqlQuery<long>($"SELECT {sequenceName}.NEXTVAL FROM DUAL").Single();
        }
    }
}

>>> FILE 48/94: Orvex.Business/Util/EfMapperExtensions.cs   [4. Business services]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Linq.Expressions;
using AutoMapper;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;

namespace Orvex.Business.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// A class which provides extensions to the Automapper registrations.
    /// Adds methods to facilitate mapping to and from entities and Transfer Objects/monitoring lists.
    /// </summary>
    public static class EfMapperExtensions
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps from List to MonitoringList
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel"></typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject"></typeparam>
        /// <param name="mapper"></param>
        /// <returns>A List of TDestination items.</returns>
        public static IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> MapListToMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>(this IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> mapper)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            Mapper.CreateMap<List<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<IEnumerable<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<IQueryable<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<ObservableCollection<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            Mapper.CreateMap<IOrderedEnumerable<TModel>, MonitoringList<TTransferObject>>()
                .ConvertUsing(CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>);
            return mapper;
        }

        private static MonitoringList<TTransferObject> CreateMonitoringList<TModel, TTransferObject>(IEnumerable<TModel> source)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            try
            {
                return new MonitoringList<TTransferObject>(source.Select(Mapper.Map<TModel, TTransferObject>));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Adds standard MonitoringList converter to a mapping from TO to Model.
        /// Calls SaveMonitoringList on the uow.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the items transfer objects.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the items in the TList.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="mapper">The mapper expression to extend.</param>
        /// <param name="modelKeySelector">The model key selector.</param>
        /// <param name="transferObjectKeySelector">The transfer object key selector.</param>
        /// <returns>
        /// A mapper expression to allow the fluent API to continue.
        /// </returns>
        public static IMappingExpression<TTransferObject, TModel> MapIntoUnitOfWork<TTransferObject, TModel>(
            this IMappingExpression<TTransferObject, TModel> mapper,
            Expression<Func<TModel, object>> modelKeySelector,
            Expression<Func<TTransferObject, object>> transferObjectKeySelector)
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
            where TModel : class
        {
            var keySelector = new UnitOfWorkTransferObjectExtensions.KeySelector<TTransferObject, TModel>(transferObjectKeySelector, modelKeySelector);
            UnitOfWorkTransferObjectExtensions.SetKeySelector(keySelector);
            Mapper.CreateMap<MonitoringList<TTransferObject>, ICollection<TModel>>()
                .ConvertUsing<MonitoringListConverter<TTransferObject, TModel>>();
            return mapper;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Ignores all destination properties which are not mapped either explicitly or by convention.
        /// Stops multiple maps into the same destination object overwriting previous maps.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TSource">The type of the source.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TDestination">The type of the destination.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="expression">The expression.</param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public static IMappingExpression<TSource, TDestination> IgnoreUnMapped<TSource, TDestination>(this IMappingExpression<TSource, TDestination> expression)
        {
            TypeMap map = Mapper.FindTypeMapFor<TSource, TDestination>();

            foreach (var property in map.GetUnmappedPropertyNames())
            {
                expression.ForMember(property, opt => opt.Ignore());
            }

            return expression;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Ignores all destination properties which would otherwise be mapped by convention.
        /// To override this for a single property, add the MapFrom BEFORE calling this method.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TSource">The type of the source.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TDestination">The type of the destination.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="expression">The expression.</param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public static IMappingExpression<TSource, TDestination> IgnoreMapByConvention<TSource, TDestination>(this IMappingExpression<TSource, TDestination> expression)
        {
            TypeMap map = Mapper.FindTypeMapFor<TSource, TDestination>();

            foreach (var property in map.GetCustomPropertyMaps().Where(p => p.CustomExpression == null).Select(p => p.DestinationProperty.Name))
            {
                expression.ForMember(property, opt => opt.Ignore());
            }

            return expression;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Adds automapper mappings to initialise the transfer object and instruct automapper how to map into monitoring lists.
        /// </summary>
        /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
        /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the transfer object.</typeparam>
        /// <param name="mapper">The mapper expression to extend.</param>
        /// <returns>A mapper expression to allow the fluent API to continue.</returns>
        public static IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> InitialiseTransferObject<TModel, TTransferObject>(this IMappingExpression<TModel, TTransferObject> mapper)
            where TModel : class
            where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        {
            mapper.AfterMap((source, dest) =>
            {
                dest.GenerateLock();
                dest.ResetChangeTracking();
            })
            .MapListToMonitoringList();

            return mapper;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Convert a MonitoringList to the collection type used by Entity Framework.
    /// Calls SaveMonitoringList on the uow.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="TTransferObject">The type of the items in the monitoringList.</typeparam>
    /// <typeparam name="TModel">The type of the model.</typeparam>
    /// <remarks>Assumes Map call provides the Unit Of Work in the options.</remarks>
    public class MonitoringListConverter<TTransferObject, TModel> : ITypeConverter<MonitoringList<TTransferObject>, ICollection<TModel>>
        where TTransferObject : AbstractTO
        where TModel : class
    {
        private const string UowKey = "uow";
        private const string SourceKey = "source";
        private const string CheckLockKey = "checkLock";
        private const string CascadeDeleteKey = "CascadeDelete";

        public ICollection<TModel> Convert(ResolutionContext context)
        {
            try
            {
                var source = context.SourceValue as MonitoringList<TTransferObject>;
                if (source == null)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentNullException(SourceKey);
                }

                var uow = context.Options.Items[UowKey] as IUnitOfWork;
                if (uow == null)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentNullException(UowKey);
                }

                bool checkLock = true;
                if (context.Options.Items.ContainsKey(CheckLockKey))
                {
                    checkLock = (bool)context.Options.Items[CheckLockKey];
                }

                if (context.Options.Items.ContainsKey(CascadeDeleteKey))
                {
                    var cascadeDelete = (bool)context.Options.Items[CascadeDeleteKey];
                    if (cascadeDelete)
                    {
                        source.GetAddedAndExisting<TTransferObject>().ForEach(to => source.Remove(to));
                    }
                }

                return uow.SaveMonitoringListInternalUseOnly<TTransferObject, TModel>(source, checkLock: checkLock);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw ex.ToKnownException();
            }
        }
    }
}
<<< END FILE 48/94: Orvex.Business/Util/EfMapperExtensions.cs

>>> FILE 49/94: Orvex.Data/EntityFrameworkRepository.cs   [5. Data layer (EF6 context, repository, unit of work, mappings)]

using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data.Entity;
using System.Linq;
using System.Linq.Expressions;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;

namespace Orvex.Data
{
    public class EntityFrameworkRepository<T> : IRepository<T> where T : class
    {
        private readonly DbContext context;
        private readonly IDatabaseExceptionParser databaseExceptionParser;
        private readonly DbSet<T> dbSet;

        public EntityFrameworkRepository(DbContext context, IDatabaseExceptionParser databaseExceptionParser)
        {
            this.context = context;
            this.databaseExceptionParser = databaseExceptionParser;
            dbSet = context.Set<T>();
        }

        #region IQueryable Interface

        public IEnumerator<T> GetEnumerator()
        {
            try
            {
                return ((IDbSet<T>)dbSet).GetEnumerator();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
        {
            try
            {
                return ((IDbSet<T>)dbSet).GetEnumerator();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public Type ElementType
        {
            get
            {
                try
                {
                    return ((IDbSet<T>)dbSet).ElementType;
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
                }
            }
        }

        public Expression Expression
        {
            get
            {
                try
                {
                    return ((IDbSet<T>)dbSet).Expression;
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
                }
            }
        }

        public IQueryProvider Provider
        {
            get
            {
                try
                {
                    return ((IDbSet<T>)dbSet).Provider;
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
                }
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region IRepository Interface

        public virtual void Delete(object id)
        {
            try
            {
                T entityToDelete = dbSet.Find(id);
                Delete(entityToDelete);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public virtual void Delete(T entityToDelete)
        {
            try
            {
                if (context.Entry(entityToDelete).State == EntityState.Detached)
                {
                    dbSet.Attach(entityToDelete);
                }

                dbSet.Remove(entityToDelete);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public virtual T GetById(object id)
        {
            try
            {
                return dbSet.Find(id);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public virtual T Insert(T entity)
        {
            try
            {                
                return dbSet.Add(entity);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }


        public virtual IEnumerable<T> InsertRange(IEnumerable<T> entity)
        {
            try
            {               
                return dbSet.AddRange(entity);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public virtual IQueryable<T> Local()
        {
            return dbSet.Local.AsQueryable();
        }

        public T Refresh(T entity)
        {
            try
            {
                context.Entry(entity).Reload();
                return entity;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public IEnumerable<T> SqlQuery(string sql, params object[] parameters)
        {
            try
            {
                return dbSet.SqlQuery(sql, parameters);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public virtual T Update(T entityToUpdate)
        {
            try
            {
                dbSet.Attach(entityToUpdate);
                context.Entry(entityToUpdate).State = EntityState.Modified;
                return entityToUpdate;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public T Update(T entityToUpdate, string[] changedPropertyNames)
        {
            try
            {
                dbSet.Attach(entityToUpdate);
                var changeTracker = context.Entry(entityToUpdate);
                changeTracker.State = EntityState.Unchanged;
                foreach (var propertyName in changedPropertyNames)
                {
                    //which is good as we want to know about it
                    changeTracker.Property(propertyName).IsModified = true;
                }

                return entityToUpdate;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region IQueryable Extension methods

        public IQueryable<T> AsNoTracking(IQueryable<T> query)
        {
            try
            {
                return query.AsNoTracking();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public IQueryable<T> AsNoTracking()
        {
            try
            {
                return dbSet.AsNoTracking();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public IQueryable<T> Include(params string[] paths)
        {
            try
            {
                IQueryable<T> query = dbSet;

                for (int i = 0; i < paths.GetLength(0); i++)
                {
                    query = query.Include(paths[i]);
                }

                return query;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public IQueryable<T> Include(params Expression<Func<T, object>>[] paths)
        {
            try
            {
                IQueryable<T> query = dbSet;

                for (int i = 0; i < paths.GetLength(0); i++)
                {
                    query = query.Include(paths[i]);
                }

                return query;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        #endregion
    }
}

====================================================================================================
>>> FILE 50/94: Orvex.Data/EntityFrameworkUnitOfWork.cs   [5. Data layer (EF6 context, repository, unit of work, mappings)]
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.Common;
using System.Data.Entity;
using System.ServiceModel;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Oracle.ManagedDataAccess.Client;

namespace Orvex.Data
{
    public class EntityFrameworkUnitOfWork : IUnitOfWork
    {
        private bool isDisposed;
        private readonly DbContext context;
        private readonly IDatabaseExceptionParser databaseExceptionParser;
        private readonly Dictionary<Type, object> repositories = new Dictionary<Type, object>();
        private readonly object syncObject = new object();
        private readonly Stack<ITransaction> transactions = new Stack<ITransaction>();

        public EntityFrameworkUnitOfWork(DbContext context, IDatabaseExceptionParser databaseExceptionParser)
        {
            this.context = context;
            this.databaseExceptionParser = databaseExceptionParser;
        }

        #region IDisposable Interface

        public void Dispose()
        {
            Dispose(true);
            GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
        }

        protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (!isDisposed)
            {
                if (disposing)
                {
                    while (transactions.Count > 0)
                    {
                        transactions.Pop().Dispose();
                    }
                }
            }

            isDisposed = true;
        }

        #endregion

        #region IUnitOfWork Interface

        public ITransaction BeginTransaction()
        {
            try
            {
                var trans = new EntityFrameworkTransaction(context.Database.Connection.BeginTransaction(IsolationLevel.ReadCommitted));
                transactions.Push(trans);
                return trans;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public void Commit()
        {
            try
            {
                context.SaveChanges();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public int ExecuteSqlCommand(string sql, params object[] parameters)
        {
            try
            {
                return context.Database.ExecuteSqlCommand(sql, parameters);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public IRepository<T> Repository<T>() where T : class
        {
            try
            {
                object repository;

                lock (syncObject)
                {
                    var entityType = typeof(T);
                    if (!repositories.TryGetValue(entityType, out repository))
                    {
                        repository = new EntityFrameworkRepository<T>(context, databaseExceptionParser);
                        repositories.Add(entityType, repository);
                    }
                }

                return (IRepository<T>)repository;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public IEnumerable<T> SqlQuery<T>(string sql, params object[] parameters)
        {
            try
            {
                return context.Database.SqlQuery<T>(sql, parameters);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw databaseExceptionParser.ToKnownException(ex);
            }
        }

        public DbConnection GetConnection()
        {
            return context.Database.Connection as OracleConnection;
        }

        #endregion
    }
}
<<< END FILE 50/94: Orvex.Data/EntityFrameworkUnitOfWork.cs

using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.Data.Common;
using System.Data.Entity;
using System.Data.Entity.Core.Metadata.Edm;
using System.Data.Entity.Infrastructure;
using System.Data.Entity.ModelConfiguration.Conventions;
using System.Reflection;
using Orvex.Data.Mappings;
using Orvex.Model;
using OrvexBusinessTierCommon.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;

namespace Orvex.Data
{
    public partial class HlxContext : DbContext
    {
        static HlxContext()
        {
            Database.SetInitializer<HlxContext>(null);
        }

        public HlxContext()
            : base()
        {
        }

        public HlxContext(DbConnection existingConnection, bool contextOwnsConnection)
            : base(existingConnection, contextOwnsConnection)
        {
            Configuration.LazyLoadingEnabled = false;
            this.Configuration.ProxyCreationEnabled = false;
            Database.Log = s => System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(s);
        }
        public DbSet<HlxAppointment> HlxAppointment { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildExamination> ChildExamination { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildExaminationGiven> ChildExaminationGiven { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildExaminationKdp> ChildExaminationKdp { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildGeneral> ChildGeneral { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildLocations> ChildLocations { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildMeasurement> ChildMeasurement { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildPreschoolRecall> ChildPreschoolRecall { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ChildSchoolRecall> ChildSchoolRecall { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Consent> Consents { get; set; }
        public DbSet<DeviationsCentile> DeviationsCentile { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ExaminationDescriptions> ExaminationDescriptions { get; set; }
        public DbSet<Examiner> Examiners { get; set; }
        public DbSet<ExamNotExaminedReason> ExamNotExaminedReason { get; set; }
        public DbSet<KdpLocationTypes> KdpLocationTypes { get; set; }        
        public DbSet<HealthCareProfessionals> HealthCareProfessionals { get; set; }
        public DbSet<PreschoolScheduleExams> PreschoolScheduleExams { get; set; }
        public DbSet<SystemGeneralOptions> SystemGeneralOptions { get; set; }

        protected override void OnModelCreating(DbModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            modelBuilder.HasDefaultSchema("APPDATA1");

            modelBuilder.Conventions.Remove<OneToManyCascadeDeleteConvention>();

            modelBuilder
                .Properties()
                .Where(p => p.PropertyType == typeof (string))
                .Configure(p => p.IsUnicode(false));

            modelBuilder.Configurations
                .AddFromAssembly(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());

            modelBuilder.Conventions.Add<PropRenamingConvention>();
            modelBuilder.Conventions.Add<TableRenamingConvention>();

        }
    }


    class PropRenamingConvention : IStoreModelConvention<EdmProperty>
    {
        public void Apply(EdmProperty property, DbModel model)
        {
            property.Name = property.Name.CamelToUpperUnderscored();
        }
    }

    class TableRenamingConvention : IStoreModelConvention<EntitySet>
    {
        public void Apply(EntitySet member, DbModel model)
        {
            member.Table = member.Table.CamelToUpperUnderscored();
        }
    }
}

using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using System.Data.Entity.ModelConfiguration;
using Orvex.Model;

namespace Orvex.Data.Mappings
{
    public class PreschoolScheduleExamsMap : EntityTypeConfiguration<PreschoolScheduleExams>
    {
        public PreschoolScheduleExamsMap()
        {
            // Primary Key
            this.HasKey(t => t.PkScheduleExamId);

            // Properties
            this.Property(t => t.PkScheduleExamId)
                .HasDatabaseGeneratedOption(DatabaseGeneratedOption.None);

            this.Property(t => t.FkExamCode)
                .IsRequired()
                .HasMaxLength(4);

            this.Property(t => t.FkExaminerId)
                .HasMaxLength(2);

            this.Property(t => t.ParentGuardianLabelsRqrd)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.PhysicalTest)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.VisionTest)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.HearingTest)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.LocomotionTest)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.ManipulationTest)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.SpeechLangTest)
                .IsFixedLength()

            this.Property(t => t.BehaviourTest)
                .IsFixedLength()

            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest1Name)

            this.Property(t => t.HomeAssessment)
                .IsFixedLength()
                .HasMaxLength(1);

            this.Property(t => t.RecordStatus)
                .HasMaxLength(1);


            // Table & Column Mappings
            this.ToTable("PRESCHOOL_SCHEDULE_EXAMS", "APPDATA1");
            this.Property(t => t.PkScheduleExamId).HasColumnName("PK_SCHEDULE_EXAM_ID");
