            this.Property(t => t.FkExaminerId).HasColumnName("FK_EXAMINER_ID");
            this.Property(t => t.ActiveFrom).HasColumnName("ACTIVE_FROM");
            this.Property(t => t.ActiveTo).HasColumnName("ACTIVE_TO");
            this.Property(t => t.MinimumAgeWks).HasColumnName("MINIMUM_AGE_WKS");
            this.Property(t => t.MaximumAgeWks).HasColumnName("MAXIMUM_AGE_WKS");
            this.Property(t => t.MinimumIntervalWks).HasColumnName("MINIMUM_INTERVAL_WKS");
            this.Property(t => t.DobFrom).HasColumnName("DOB_FROM");
            this.Property(t => t.DobTo).HasColumnName("DOB_TO");
            this.Property(t => t.ParentGuardianLabelsRqrd).HasColumnName("PARENT_GUARDIAN_LABELS_RQRD");
            this.Property(t => t.PhysicalTest).HasColumnName("PHYSICAL_TEST");
            this.Property(t => t.VisionTest).HasColumnName("VISION_TEST");
            this.Property(t => t.HearingTest).HasColumnName("HEARING_TEST");
            this.Property(t => t.LocomotionTest).HasColumnName("LOCOMOTION_TEST");
            this.Property(t => t.ManipulationTest).HasColumnName("MANIPULATION_TEST");
            this.Property(t => t.SpeechLangTest).HasColumnName("SPEECH_LANG_TEST");
            this.Property(t => t.BehaviourTest).HasColumnName("BEHAVIOUR_TEST");

            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest1).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST1");
            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest2Name).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST2_NAME");
            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest2).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST2");
            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest3Name).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST3_NAME");
            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest3).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST3");
            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest4Name).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST4_NAME");
            this.Property(t => t.LocalTest4).HasColumnName("LOCAL_TEST4");
            this.Property(t => t.HomeAssessment).HasColumnName("HOME_ASSESSMENT");
            this.Property(t => t.RecordStatus).HasColumnName("RECORD_STATUS");

            // Relationships
            this.HasRequired(t => t.ExaminationDescriptions)
                .WithMany(t => t.PreschoolScheduleExams)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.FkExamCode);
            this.HasRequired(t => t.Examiner)
                .WithMany(t => t.PreschoolScheduleExams)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.FkExaminerId);
        }
    }

        public string IsCancelled { get; set; }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using HLX.BusinessTier.Common.Util;
using Orvex.Business.Validation;
using Orvex.HLX.BusinessTier.Common.AutoMapperConfig;
using Orvex.Contracts.Enums;
using Orvex.Model;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using FluentAssertions;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using Moq;

namespace Orvex.Business.Tests
{
    [TestClass]
    public class KdpExaminationServiceTests
    {
        #region Private fields

        private const long InvalidClientId = 111111;
        private const long ValidClientId = 1234674927;
        private const long AppointmentId = 999;
        private const string ValidExamCodePrefix = "KD";
        private const int ConsentTypePreschoolAudio = 13;
        private const int ConsentTypePreschoolVision = 12;
        private const int ConsentTypePreschoolMedical = 11;
        private const string ChildExamIdSequenceSql = "SELECT CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID_SEQ.NEXTVAL FROM DUAL";

        private readonly DateTime currentDateTime = new DateTime(2016, 2, 1, 14, 02, 57);

        private KdpExaminationService kdpExaminationService;
        private ChildExamination[] childExaminationList;
        private KdpLocationTypes[] kdpLocationTypesList;
        private DateTime originalConsentDate;

        private KdpExaminationValidator kdpExaminationValidator;
        private Mock<IUnitOfWork> mockUnitOfWork;
        private ICollection<Consent> consents;

        #endregion

        [TestInitialize]
        public void Initialize()
        {
            originalConsentDate = currentDateTime.Date.AddDays(-7);

            consents = GetConsents();

            mockUnitOfWork = new Mock<IUnitOfWork>();
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(consents);
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(GetAppointments());
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(new List<ChildExaminationGiven>());
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(new List<ChildExaminationKdp>());
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(new []{ new ChildGeneral { ClientId = ValidClientId }});
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(new List<ChildPreschoolRecall>());
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(new List<ChildSchoolRecall>());


            mockUnitOfWork.Setup(u => u.SqlQuery<long>(ChildExamIdSequenceSql)).Returns(new List<long> { 1 });
            childExaminationList = GetValidChildExamLocation();
            kdpLocationTypesList = GetLocationTypes();
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(childExaminationList);
            mockUnitOfWork.InitialiseRepository(kdpLocationTypesList);

            kdpExaminationValidator = new KdpExaminationValidator();

            var getCurrentDateTime = new GetCurrentDateTime(() => currentDateTime);

            this.kdpExaminationService = new KdpExaminationService(() => this.mockUnitOfWork.Object,
                kdpExaminationValidator, getCurrentDateTime);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void GetKdpExamResult_GivenValidId_ReturnsData()
        {
            // Arrange
            AutoMapperConfiguration.Configure();

            // Act
            var kdpExaminationResults = kdpExaminationService.GetKdpExamResults(ValidClientId);

            // Assert
            kdpExaminationResults.Should().HaveCount(4);
            kdpExaminationResults.All(item => item.ClientId == ValidClientId).Should().BeTrue();
            kdpExaminationResults.Should().BeInDescendingOrder(item => item.ExaminationDate);
            kdpExaminationResults.All(item => item.ExaminationCode.Contains(ValidExamCodePrefix)).Should().BeTrue();
            kdpExaminationResults.Any(item => item.ExamDescription == null).Should().BeFalse();
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void GetKdpExamResult_GivenInvalidId_ThrowsInvalidOperationException()
        {
            // Arrange
            AutoMapperConfiguration.Configure();

            // Act
            Action kdpEWxamResultsAction = () => kdpExaminationService.GetKdpExamResults(InvalidClientId);

            // Assert
            kdpEWxamResultsAction.ShouldThrow<InvalidOperationException>();
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void SaveScheduledResult_GivenWithdrawConsentIsTrue_ShouldSetMedicalConsent()
        {
            // Arrange
            var kdpTo = new KdpScheduledResultTO
            {
                ClientId = ValidClientId,
                ExaminationCode = "CODE",
                ExaminationDate = new DateTime(2016,1,1),
                AppointmentId = AppointmentId,
                ReasonNotExamined = "1",
                WithdrawConsent = true,
                PkChildExaminationId = 0,
                LocationType = 1
            };
            
            KdpExaminationService.ConfigureMappings();

            // Act
            kdpExaminationService.SaveScheduledResult(kdpTo);

            // Assert
            consents.Where(c => c.ConsentType == ConsentTypePreschoolAudio
                && c.ConsentResult == LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT]
                && c.DateOfConsent == originalConsentDate).Should().HaveCount(1);

            consents.Where(c => c.ConsentType == ConsentTypePreschoolVision
                && c.ConsentResult == LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT]
                && c.DateOfConsent == originalConsentDate).Should().HaveCount(1);

            consents.Where(c => c.ConsentType == ConsentTypePreschoolMedical
                && c.ConsentResult == LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT_REFUSED]
                && c.DateOfConsent == currentDateTime.Date).Should().HaveCount(1);

            mockUnitOfWork.Verify(u => u.Commit(), Times.Once);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void SaveScheduledResult_GivenWithdrawConsentIsFalse_ShouldNotSetMedicalConsent()
        {
            // Arrange
            var kdpTo = new KdpScheduledResultTO
            {
                ClientId = ValidClientId,
                ExaminationCode = "CODE",
                ExaminationDate = new DateTime(2016, 1, 1),
                AppointmentId = AppointmentId,
                ReasonNotExamined = "1",
                WithdrawConsent = false,
                PkChildExaminationId = 0,
                LocationType = 1
            };
            
            KdpExaminationService.ConfigureMappings();

            // Act
            kdpExaminationService.SaveScheduledResult(kdpTo);

            // Assert
            consents.Any(c => c.ConsentResult != LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT]
                || c.DateOfConsent != originalConsentDate).Should().BeFalse();

            mockUnitOfWork.Verify(u => u.Commit(), Times.Once);
        }

        private static ChildExamination[] GetValidChildExamLocation()
        {
            var childExams = new[]
            {
                new ChildExamination
                {
                    PkChildExaminationId = 5845,
                    ClientId = ValidClientId,
                    Sector = "01",

                    {
                        new ChildMeasurement
                        {
                            FkChildExaminationId = 5845,
                            Height = 60,
                            Weight = 10,
                            HeadCircumference = 40
                        }
                    }
                },
                new ChildExamination
                {
                    PkChildExaminationId = 5847,
                    ClientId = ValidClientId,
                    Sector = "01",
                    FkExaminationId = "KD03",
                    Origin = 2,
                    ExaminationDescriptions = GetExaminationDescriptions("KD03"),
                    ChildExaminationGiven = GetChildExaminationGiven(5853, new DateTime(2016, 08, 03)),
                    ChildMeasurement = new[]
                    {
                        new ChildMeasurement
                        {
                            FkChildExaminationId = 5847,
                            Height = 50,
                            Weight = 8,
                            HeadCircumference = 38
                        }
                    }
                },
                new ChildExamination
                {
                    PkChildExaminationId = 5853,
                    ClientId = ValidClientId,
                    Sector = "01",
                    FkExaminationId = "KD08",
                    Origin = 2,
                    ExaminationDescriptions = GetExaminationDescriptions("KD08"),
                    ChildExaminationGiven = GetChildExaminationGiven(5853, new DateTime(2016, 08, 04))
                },
                new ChildExamination

                    PkChildExaminationId = 5857,
                    ClientId = ValidClientId,
                    Sector = "01",
                    FkExaminationId = "KD11",
                    Origin = 2,
                    ExaminationDescriptions = GetExaminationDescriptions("KD11"),
                    ChildExaminationGiven = GetChildExaminationGiven(5845, new DateTime(2016, 08, 03))
                }
            };
            return childExams;
        }

        private static ChildExaminationGiven GetChildExaminationGiven(long id, DateTime examDate, byte? locationType = null)
        {

            {
                PkFkChildExamGivenId = id,
                ExaminationDate = examDate,
                FkLocationHeldTypeId = locationType,
                ChildExaminationKdp = new ChildExaminationKdp
                {
                    PkFkExamKdpGivenId = id,
                    Consent = "Y",
                    EvidenceOfXgm = 4,
                    DomesticViolenceConcern = 3,
                    OnwardReferralsMade = "Y",
                    Heart = 1,
                    Development = 1,
                    DskLocomotor = "20"
                }
            };
        }

        private static ExaminationDescriptions GetExaminationDescriptions(string examId)
        {
            return new ExaminationDescriptions
            {
                PkExamDescriptionId = examId,
                FkExamDescriptionType = ExamType.Kdp,
                LongDescription = "Test Long"
            };
        }

                    FkLocationType = 6,

        }

        private ICollection<Consent> GetConsents()
        {
            return new List<Consent>
            {
                new Consent
                {
                    ClientId = ValidClientId,
                    ConsentCategory = (byte)LookupsUtility.CONSENT_CATEGORY.PSH,
                    ConsentType = ConsentTypePreschoolAudio,

                    ConsentResult = LookupsUtility.ExamConsentResultMap()[LookupsUtility.EXAM_CONSENT_RESULTS.CONSENT],

                }

        }

using Orvex.Business.Validation;
using Orvex.Services;
using Orvex.Services.Internal;
using Orvex.TransferObjects;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using Moq;
using FluentAssertions;

namespace Orvex.ViewModels.Tests
{
    [TestClass]
    public class KdpExaminationResultDelegateTests
    {
        private KdpExaminationValidator validator;
        private Mock<IMessenger> messenger;
        private Mock<ILookupService> mockLookupService;
        private Mock<IChildMeasurementsService> mockChildMeasurementService;
        private Mock<IKdpExaminationService> mockKdpExaminationService;
        private KdpExaminationResultDelegate subjectUnderTest;

        [TestInitialize]
        public void Initialize()
        {
            validator = new KdpExaminationValidator();
            messenger = new Mock<IMessenger>();
            mockLookupService = new Mock<ILookupService>();
            mockChildMeasurementService = new Mock<IChildMeasurementsService>();
            mockKdpExaminationService = new Mock<IKdpExaminationService>();
            subjectUnderTest = new KdpExaminationResultDelegate(validator, messenger.Object, mockLookupService.Object,
                mockChildMeasurementService.Object, mockKdpExaminationService.Object)
            {
                KdpExaminationResultTo = new KdpExaminationResultTO()
            };
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void IsHeightDeclined_GivenTrue_RaisesPropertyChanged()
        {
            // Arrange
            subjectUnderTest.MonitorEvents();

            // Act
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeightDeclined = true;

            // Assert
            subjectUnderTest.ShouldRaisePropertyChangeFor(x => x.IsHeightRequired);
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeightRequired.Should().Be(false);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void IsHeightDeclined_GivenFalse_RaisesPropertyChanged()
        {
            // Arrange
            subjectUnderTest.MonitorEvents();

            // Act
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeightDeclined = false;

            // Assert
            subjectUnderTest.ShouldRaisePropertyChangeFor(x => x.IsHeightRequired);
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeightRequired.Should().Be(true);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void IsWeightDeclined_GivenTrue_RaisesPropertyChanged()
        {
            // Arrange
            subjectUnderTest.MonitorEvents();

            // Act
            subjectUnderTest.IsWeightDeclined = true;

            // Assert

        public void IsWeightDeclined_GivenFalse_RaisesPropertyChanged()

            // Arrange

            // Act
            subjectUnderTest.IsWeightDeclined = false;

            // Assert
            subjectUnderTest.ShouldRaisePropertyChangeFor(x => x.IsWeightRequired);
            subjectUnderTest.IsWeightRequired.Should().Be(true);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void IsHeadCircumferenceDeclined_GivenTrue_RaisesPropertyChanged()
        {
            // Arrange
            subjectUnderTest.MonitorEvents();

            // Act
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeadCircumferenceDeclined = true;

            // Assert
            subjectUnderTest.ShouldRaisePropertyChangeFor(x => x.IsHeadCircumferenceRequired);
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeadCircumferenceRequired.Should().Be(false);
        }

        [TestMethod]

        {

            // Act
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeadCircumferenceDeclined = false;

            // Assert
            subjectUnderTest.ShouldRaisePropertyChangeFor(x => x.IsHeadCircumferenceRequired);
            subjectUnderTest.IsHeadCircumferenceRequired.Should().Be(true);

>>> FILE 94/94: KDP_Schema.sql   [8. SQL (schema and seed data)]
====================================================================================================
-- KDP schema (Oracle, owner APPDATA1)
-- Reconstructed from the Entity Framework models/mappings in Orvex.Model and
-- Orvex.Data/Mappings. It is NOT a dump of a live database:
--   * NUMBER precision is inferred from CLR types (long -> NUMBER(18), byte -> NUMBER(3),
--     short -> NUMBER(5)) unless the mapping states a precision.
--   * Unstated VARCHAR2 lengths are marked "-- length not in mapping (VARCHAR2 size below is a placeholder)".
--   * Constraint names and indexes are illustrative; check the real DB before reusing.
--   * Triggers (e.g. SECTOR, which EF treats as DB-computed) are not shown.

-- =====================================================================
-- 1. Tables written by KdpExaminationService.SaveKdpExaminationResult
--    (InsertKdpExaminationResult / UpdateKdpExaminationResult / Delete)
-- =====================================================================

-- Parent record for every examination (KDP exam code in FK_EXAMINATION_ID, e.g. KD01..KD11)
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION (
    PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID     NUMBER(18)    NOT NULL,   -- from CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID_SEQ
    CLIENT_ID                   NUMBER(18)    NOT NULL,   -- FK CHILD_GENERAL
    SECTOR                      VARCHAR2(2)   NOT NULL,   -- DB-computed
    FK_EXAMINATION_ID           VARCHAR2(4)   NOT NULL,   -- FK EXAMINATION_DESCRIPTIONS
    FK_AGENCY_REFERRED_FROM_ID  VARCHAR2(3),
    ORIGIN                      NUMBER(3),                -- LookupsUtility.EXAMINATION_ORIGIN
    IS_REPEAT                   VARCHAR2(1),              -- Y/N, maintained by ExaminationsCrudManager.UpdateRepeatFlags; column name comes from the camel-to-UPPER_UNDERSCORE store convention in HlxContext
    RECORD_STATUS               VARCHAR2(1),
    CONSTRAINT PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION PRIMARY KEY (PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID)
);

-- 1:1 with CHILD_EXAMINATION. Where, when, who, and why-not-examined
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION_GIVEN (
    PK_FK_CHILD_EXAM_GIVEN_ID       NUMBER(18)   NOT NULL,   -- = CHILD_EXAMINATION.PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID
    EXAMINATION_DATE                DATE         NOT NULL,
    EXAMINATION_DATE_ESTIMATED      VARCHAR2(1),
    FK_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON_ID       NUMBER(3),               -- FK EXAM_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON
    RESCHEDULE_EXAMINATION          VARCHAR2(1)  DEFAULT 'N' NOT NULL,
    PARENT_PRESENT                  VARCHAR2(1),
    HEIGHT_MEASUREMENT_REFUSED      VARCHAR2(1)  DEFAULT 'N' NOT NULL,
    WEIGHT_MEASUREMENT_REFUSED      VARCHAR2(1)  DEFAULT 'N' NOT NULL,
    HEAD_MEASUREMENT_REFUSED        VARCHAR2(1)  DEFAULT 'N' NOT NULL,
    RECORD_STATUS                   VARCHAR2(1),
    ORIGIN                          NUMBER(3)    NOT NULL,
    FK_REFERRED_FROM_AGENCY_CODE    VARCHAR2(3),             -- FK AGENCY_CODES
    FK_LOCATION_HELD_TYPE_ID        NUMBER(3),               -- with FK_CHILD_LOCATION_HELD_CODE -> CHILD_LOCATIONS
    FK_CHILD_LOCATION_HELD_CODE     VARCHAR2(8),
    FK_STAFF_TYPE_EXAMINER_ID       VARCHAR2(2),             -- with FK_HCP_EXAMINER_CODE -> HEALTH_CARE_PROFESSIONALS
    FK_HCP_EXAMINER_CODE            VARCHAR2(8),
    FK_EXAMINER_CAT_ID              VARCHAR2(1),             -- FK EXAMINER
    FK_CURR_TREATMENT_AGENCY_CODE   VARCHAR2(3),
    RESULT_ENTERED_DATE             DATE,
    CONSTRAINT PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_GIVEN PRIMARY KEY (PK_FK_CHILD_EXAM_GIVEN_ID),
    CONSTRAINT FK_CEG_CHILD_EXAMINATION FOREIGN KEY (PK_FK_CHILD_EXAM_GIVEN_ID)
        REFERENCES APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION (PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID),
    CONSTRAINT FK_CEG_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON FOREIGN KEY (FK_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON_ID)
        REFERENCES APPDATA1.EXAM_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON (PK_EXAM_NOT_EXAM_RSON_ID)
);

-- 1:0..1 with CHILD_EXAMINATION_GIVEN. KDP-specific result fields
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION_KDP (
    PK_FK_EXAM_KDP_GIVEN_ID    NUMBER(18)   NOT NULL,       -- = CHILD_EXAMINATION_GIVEN.PK_FK_CHILD_EXAM_GIVEN_ID
    CONSENT                     VARCHAR2(1),
    ONWARD_REFERRALS_MADE       VARCHAR2(1),
    RECEIVING_BOOST_SCHEME      VARCHAR2(1),                 -- Y/N/U
    LOOKED_AFTER_CHILD          VARCHAR2(1),                 -- Y/N/U
    ON_CHILD_PROTECTION_REGISTER VARCHAR2(1),                -- Y/N/U
    EVIDENCE_OF_XGM             NUMBER(3),
    DOMESTIC_VIOLENCE_CONCERN   NUMBER(3),
    -- RSK scores (1-5); only for contacts KD01,KD04,KD07,KD08,KD09,KD10
    RSK_RESPONSIVE_PARENTING  NUMBER(3),
    RSK_ENGAGEMENT            NUMBER(3),
    RSK_SOCIO_FACTORS         NUMBER(3),
    RSK_FAMILY_HEALTH         NUMBER(3),
    RSK_FAMILY_SUPPORT        NUMBER(3),
    RSK_COMPLETED             VARCHAR2(1),
    -- 15 / 27 month contacts (KD08, KD09)
    DEVELOPMENT                 NUMBER(3),
    DEVELOPMENT_FURTHER         VARCHAR2(1),
    -- 6-8 week contact (KD03) physical assessment
    HEART                       NUMBER(3),
    HIPS                        NUMBER(3),
    EYES                        NUMBER(3),
    TESTES                      NUMBER(3),
    PHYSICAL_GENERAL            NUMBER(3),
    -- DSK scores (KD08, KD09, KD11); numeric string or 'N' = not assessed
    DSK_LOCOMOTOR              VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_MANIPULATIVE           VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_VISUAL                 VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_HEARING_AND_LANGUAGE   VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_SPEECH_AND_LANGUAGE    VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_INTERACTIVE_SOCIAL     VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_SELF_CARE_SOCIAL       VARCHAR2(2),
    DSK_COGNITIVE              VARCHAR2(2),
    RECORD_STATUS               VARCHAR2(1),
    CORE_COMPONENT              NUMBER(3),
    CONSTRAINT PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_KDP PRIMARY KEY (PK_FK_EXAM_KDP_GIVEN_ID),
    CONSTRAINT FK_CEH_CHILD_EXAM_GIVEN FOREIGN KEY (PK_FK_EXAM_KDP_GIVEN_ID)
        REFERENCES APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION_GIVEN (PK_FK_CHILD_EXAM_GIVEN_ID)
);

-- Height / weight / head circumference captured on the KDP screen (0..n per examination, 1 used here)
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.CHILD_MEASUREMENT (
    PK_CHILD_MEASUREMENT_ID     NUMBER(18)   NOT NULL,       -- from CHILD_MEASUREMENT_ID_SEQ
    CLIENT_ID                   NUMBER(18)   NOT NULL,       -- FK CHILD_GENERAL
    SECTOR                      VARCHAR2(2)  NOT NULL,       -- DB-computed
    MEASUREMENT_DATE            DATE         NOT NULL,
    HEIGHT                      NUMBER(4,1),
    HEAD_CIRCUMFERENCE          NUMBER,                      -- precision not in mapping
    OUTCOME                     NUMBER(3),
    BODY_MASS_INDEX             NUMBER,                      -- precision not in mapping
    FK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID     NUMBER(18),                  -- FK CHILD_EXAMINATION
    WEIGHT                      NUMBER(6,3),
    RECORD_STATUS               VARCHAR2(1),
    CONSTRAINT PK_CHILD_MEASUREMENT PRIMARY KEY (PK_CHILD_MEASUREMENT_ID),
    CONSTRAINT FK_CM_CHILD_EXAMINATION FOREIGN KEY (FK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID)
        REFERENCES APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION (PK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID)
);

CREATE SEQUENCE APPDATA1.CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID_SEQ  START WITH 1 INCREMENT BY 1 CACHE 20;
CREATE SEQUENCE APPDATA1.CHILD_MEASUREMENT_ID_SEQ  START WITH 1 INCREMENT BY 1 CACHE 20;

-- =====================================================================
-- 2. Tables updated as a side effect of a save
-- =====================================================================

-- ExaminationsCrudManager.UpdateAppointmentStatuses / ClearProducedRecalls / ResetAppointmentsForDeletedResult
-- Only the columns touched or read by the KDP flow are significant (EXAMINATION_DUE like 'HC%', IS_OUTSTANDING,
-- FK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID, IS_MARKED_AS_COMPLETE, IS_CANCELLED).
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.HLX_APPOINTMENT (
    PK_HLX_APPOINTMENT_ID           NUMBER(18)   NOT NULL,
    APPOINTMENT_DATETIME            DATE         NOT NULL,
    CLIENT_ID                       NUMBER(18),
    SECTOR                          VARCHAR2(2)  NOT NULL,
    FK_CHILD_LOCATIONS_SESSIONS_ID  NUMBER(18),
    RECORD_STATUS                   VARCHAR2(1),
    FAILURE_TO_ATTEND_TYPE          NUMBER(3),
    FK_APPOINTMENT_RUN_ID           NUMBER(18),
    CLINIC_LIST_PAGE                NUMBER,
    CLINIC_LIST_ROW                 NUMBER(3)    NOT NULL,
    PRIORITY                        NUMBER(3),
    APPOINTMENT_ENDTIME             DATE,
    EXAMINATION_DUE                 VARCHAR2(4),
    FK_EXAMINATION_TYPE             NUMBER(3),               -- ExamType enum (Kdp is one value)
    IS_RECALL                       NUMBER,
    IS_AUTO_GENERATED               VARCHAR2(1),
    IS_LETTER_PRODUCED              VARCHAR2(1),             -- mapping sets this column name on IsAutoGenerated too (likely a legacy bug)
    IS_OUTSTANDING                  VARCHAR2(1),
    IS_MARKED_AS_COMPLETE           VARCHAR2(1),
    IS_CANCELLED                    VARCHAR2(1),
    FK_LETTER_STATUS_CODE           VARCHAR2(4),
    LETTER_ERROR_MESSAGE            VARCHAR2(200),
    LETTER_NOTIFY_ID                VARCHAR2(200),
    FK_CHILD_EXAMINATION_ID         NUMBER(18),
    APPOINTMENT_SLOT_GROUP          NUMBER(5),
    BOOKING_SOURCE                  VARCHAR2(3),
    BOOKING_DATE                    DATE,
    DATE_ARCHIVED                   DATE,
    LAST_OFFERED                    DATE,
    CONSTRAINT PK_HLX_APPOINTMENT PRIMARY KEY (PK_HLX_APPOINTMENT_ID)
);

-- SaveScheduledResult with WithdrawConsent: PSH (preschool) category, type 11 (medical) row set to "refused"
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.CONSENTS (
    CONSENT_ID          NUMBER(18)    NOT NULL,
    CLIENT_ID           NUMBER(18)    NOT NULL,
    SECTOR              VARCHAR2(2)   NOT NULL,
    CONSENT_TYPE        NUMBER(3)     NOT NULL,
    CONSENT_CATEGORY    NUMBER(3)     NOT NULL,
    DATE_OF_CONSENT     DATE,
    REASON_NO_CONSENT   VARCHAR2(2),
    CREATION_DATE       DATE          NOT NULL,
    CREATION_ORIGIN     VARCHAR2(30)  NOT NULL,
    CREATION_USER       NUMBER(18)    NOT NULL,
    MODIFICATION_DATE   DATE          NOT NULL,
    CONSENT_RESULT      VARCHAR2(1),
    RECORD_STATUS       VARCHAR2(1),
    CONSTRAINT PK_CONSENTS PRIMARY KEY (CONSENT_ID)
);

-- Location types offered on the KDP screen; FK_LOCATION_TYPE maps to the generic location type
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.KDP_LOCATION_TYPES (
    CODE             NUMBER(3)     NOT NULL,
    DESCRIPTION      VARCHAR2(50),                           -- length not in mapping (VARCHAR2 size below is a placeholder)
    FK_LOCATION_TYPE NUMBER(3)     NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT PK_KDP_LOCATION_TYPES PRIMARY KEY (CODE)
);
-- Seed data: KDP_LOCATION_TYPES.sql

-- Reason-not-examined; KDP_DESCRIPTION is the KDP-specific wording
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.EXAM_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON (
    PK_EXAM_NOT_EXAM_RSON_ID NUMBER(3)    NOT NULL,
    DESCRIPTION              VARCHAR2(100),                  -- length not in mapping (VARCHAR2 size below is a placeholder)
    KDP_DESCRIPTION         VARCHAR2(100),                  -- length not in mapping (VARCHAR2 size below is a placeholder)
    RECORD_STATUS            VARCHAR2(1),
    PREFIX_CODE              VARCHAR2(10),                   -- length not in mapping (VARCHAR2 size below is a placeholder); possibly unused
    PREFIX                   VARCHAR2(10),                   -- length not in mapping (VARCHAR2 size below is a placeholder)
    DISPLAY_ORDER            NUMBER(3),
    CONSTRAINT PK_EXAM_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON PRIMARY KEY (PK_EXAM_NOT_EXAM_RSON_ID)
);
-- Seed data: EXAM_NOT_EXAMINED_REASON.sql

-- Age window per exam code (weeks); drives the "contact given before/after recommended age" warnings
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.PRESCHOOL_SCHEDULE_EXAMS (
    PK_SCHEDULE_EXAM_ID         NUMBER(5)    NOT NULL,
    FK_PRESCHOOL_SCHEDULE_ID    NUMBER(5)    NOT NULL,
    FK_EXAM_CODE                VARCHAR2(4),
    FK_EXAMINER_ID              VARCHAR2(1),
    ACTIVE_FROM                 DATE,
    ACTIVE_TO                   DATE,
    MINIMUM_AGE_WKS             NUMBER,
    MAXIMUM_AGE_WKS             NUMBER,
    MINIMUM_INTERVAL_WKS        NUMBER,
    DOB_FROM                    DATE,
    DOB_TO                      DATE,
    RECORD_STATUS               VARCHAR2(1),
    -- plus the *_TEST / LOCAL_TEST* / HOME_ASSESSMENT / PARENT_GUARDIAN_LABELS_RQRD flag columns (VARCHAR2(1)); see Orvex.Model/PRESCHOOL_SCHEDULE_EXAMS.cs
    CONSTRAINT PK_PRESCHOOL_SCHEDULE_EXAMS PRIMARY KEY (PK_SCHEDULE_EXAM_ID)
);

-- ExaminationsCrudManager.ClearProducedRecalls: RECORD_STATUS set to D where RECALL_INDICATOR = A (preschool) / P (school)
CREATE TABLE APPDATA1.CHILD_PRESCHOOL_RECALL (

    SECTOR                VARCHAR2(2),
    FK_EXAM_CODE          VARCHAR2(4)  NOT NULL,
    FK_EXAMINER_ID        VARCHAR2(2)  NOT NULL,
    FK_EXAM_CENTRE_CODE   VARCHAR2(8)  NOT NULL,
    FK_EXAM_CENTRE_TYPE   NUMBER(3)    NOT NULL,
    RECALL_DATE           DATE         NOT NULL,
    RECALL_INDICATOR      VARCHAR2(1),
    RECORD_STATUS         VARCHAR2(1),
    CONSTRAINT PK_CHILD_PRESCHOOL_RECALL PRIMARY KEY (FK_CLIENT_ID, FK_EXAMINER_ID, FK_EXAM_CODE, FK_EXAM_CENTRE_CODE, FK_EXAM_CENTRE_TYPE, RECALL_DATE)
);

    CLIENT_ID               NUMBER(18)  NOT NULL,

    DATE_DUE                DATE,
    EXAM_CODE               VARCHAR2(4),
    RECALL_INDICATOR        VARCHAR2(1),
    NEXT_SESSION_INDICATOR  VARCHAR2(1),
    RECORD_STATUS           VARCHAR2(1),
    SECTOR                  VARCHAR2(2),
    CONSTRAINT PK_CHILD_SCHOOL_RECALL PRIMARY KEY (PK_CHILD_SCHOOL_RECALL)
);

-- ChildMeasurementsService: expected measurement bands per age in days / gender (a view)
-- V_DEVIATIONS_CENTILES (AGE NUMBER(5), GENDER VARCHAR2, MEASUREMENT_TYPE VARCHAR2, CENTILE VARCHAR2, LOWER_BOUND NUMBER, UPPER_BOUND NUMBER)

-- Read via Include() for display names only. Key columns plus those used:
--   CHILD_LOCATIONS (CODE, LOCATION_TYPE [composite key], NAME, ACTIVE_FROM, ACTIVE_TO, RECORD_STATUS ...)
--   HEALTH_CARE_PROFESSIONALS (CODE, STAFF_TYPE [composite key], NAME1, NAME2, ACTIVE_FROM, ACTIVE_TO, RECORD_STATUS ...)
-- Stored procedures called by LookupService: LOOKUPS.GET_EXAMINER(pUsed, pExaminerType, pCode, pDataset)
--                                             LOOKUPS.GET_LOCATIONS(pUsed, pLocationType, pCode, pDataset)
-- Stored procedure called by PreschoolsManager: see PreschoolsManager.cs (next-exam-due)
